# 第 24 章　鸣笛于归寂之时

*4.4 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> In Ravages Does the Whistle Sound　·　鸣笛于归寂之时

## 203　How I Met Your Father　我如何遇到你的父亲

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Inkford Hermitage　|　角色：Asat Pramad; Graphia; Evanescia; Himeko; Mortenax Blade; Pearl; Poofy; Silver Wolf; Snowie; Trailblazer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Non-Existent State'' completed

### Story Recap

After resolving the crisis surrounding Fulwish, we returned to the Astral Express to gather our thoughts. Shuhu, Asat Pramad, and the Voracity... unforeseen challenges arrived in rapid succession. After careful deliberation, we decided to split into teams and tackle the problems in Planarcadia together.

There, Yao Guang, Dan Heng, and Welt traversed every corner of Planarcadia to deploy the "Deca-Light Reflection Barrier" as a safeguard against a potential ambush by the Lord Ravager. Meanwhile, Blade decided to set out for the edge of the World in Canvas, where he intended to use the power of the Voracity to bring his conflict with Shuhu to its final conclusion.

Elsewhere, Ashveil, March 7th and the others headed to the tavern in search of clues regarding Asat Pramad. Their investigation eventually centered on "Enroute." This former Masked Fool left the tavern for the Astral Express, becoming the Navigator known as "Isee". And the Voracity shadow of Ashveil is, in fact, his "legacy."

In addition, Ruan Mei brought word that the Phantasmoon Games were more than a game. They were a ritual enacted by Aha to suppress the dead husk of the Voracity. She further suggested that the Path of Elation may have originated from a concept stripped away from the Voracity: "Aftertaste."

At the same time, I ventured into the World in Canvas in search of Graphia at Evanescia's request. By a twist of fate, I truly did encounter the Mother of Imagenesis. She spoke of the Cindearth Age, of the day the Express fell, and of her stand against Asat/the Asat Pramad, before beckoning me further into the World in Canvas.

Elsewhere, Himeko returned to Graphia Academy to accompany Ryusuke, probing him for any ties to the Destruction. As they revisited their shared memories, Ryusuke posed a chilling theory: That the real Himeko had long ago succumbed to the Weathering Curse, and the person standing before him was merely an imagenae she had painted to carry on her dream of Trailblazing.

On the banks of the "Inkford Hermitage," I crossed paths with Silver Wolf and Blade once more. There, I summoned Graphia, who ferried us into the depths of the World in Canvas. In the Meadow on the Distant Shore, Blade bid us farewell and threw himself resolutely into the Voracity's maw. Embracing the path he had walked, he reclaimed his heart as a craftsman. By harnessing the Voracity's furnace-like destructive power and the regenerative flesh of the Abundance, he vowed to temper himself into the "Blade" that would imprison Shuhu for eternity.

In the Meadow on the Distant Shore, it dawned on me that the "Graphia" we saw earlier at Inkford Hermitage was an impostor. The true culprit, Asat Pramad, revealed himself, exposing that Graphia is in fact a scion of Destruction. He then broke our assault, grievously wounding Graphia, before he departed.

As we struggled through a grueling battle against the maddened Graphia, Blade returned from his nirvana. Having tempered Shuhu within himself, he joined us to turn the tide, and with his aid, Graphia was restored to her senses. She revealed that Asat Pramad could seemingly manifest in many places at once. It was exactly as she said: And indeed, at that very same time, Ashveil, Yao Guang, and the others were also attacked by Asat Pramad.

Worst of all, even Ryusuke, Himeko's father, had been Asat Pramad in disguise from the very beginning. After revealing himself, he stood before us all and declared that an unpredictable disaster was about to descend upon Planarcadia...

### Follow the clues left by Evanescia to find your companions

> **爻光：**星核猎手…当他们是友非敌时可是强援了，有他去接应，我也好更专注于当下的局面了。

**Yao Guang:** "Azure One, Vermilion Five, aligned Sky, come forth." Help me find that Hunter.

> **爻光：**「苍一，朱五，乾方偏位」，拜托，帮我找到那位猎手。

Having heeded the whispers of Yao Guang, the bird flapped its wings and soared deeper into the ruins...

**Yao Guang:** I'll try sharing the location of the talisman with Blade. Hopefully, the message will reach him safely.

> **爻光：**我会试着和刃共享符箓的位置，希望这份消息能平安送到他的手中。

**March 7th:** The unbreakable March will pull herself together too! I'll go check on the buried Express.

> **三月七：**打不倒的小三月也要振作起来了，我先去看看列车掩埋的情况。

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), we'll be counting on you to reassure everyone!

> **三月七：**开拓者，安抚大家就拜托你了！

### Check on the situation inside Perdido Station (0/4)

*(Go to mission target)*

**Melancholic Survivor:** Mommy didn't make it out...

**Helpless Survivor:** Will somebody come and help us!

**IPC Security Mechatron:** "Warning: Power levels critical. Non-combat modules have been deactivated."

**Despairing Survivor:** It's dying too? We're done for, game over!

**Bewildered Survivor:** I was trapped on the street, then a talking monkey saved me!

**Clear-Headed Survivor:** Is it possible you hit your head on something?

*(Idle lines)*

Hey chat! I'm live from the heart of the Demon Capital! If I survive this, I'll be the number one streamer! Let's get it!

Taking bets on how long the streamer can survive!

No, no, this definitely isn't Duomension City... Let me go back! I just want to go back!

I quit! Send me back!

*(Listen to the crowd, optional)*

**Worried Survivor:** With this many people, how long can the station's supplies even last...

**Calm Survivor:** Try to conserve what you have. Talk less, sleep more.

**(Trailblazer):** Perdido Station is fully stocked with supplies, so rest assured, everyone!

**Pessimistic Survivor:** The city is destroyed already. This so-called refuge is nothing but a futile attempt to delay the inevitable.

**Uneasy Survivor:** But... we've gotta do something, right?

**(Trailblazer):** Don't give up! So long as we stand together in solidarity and keep each other under protection, there will always be hope!

**Despondent Survivor:** Half a lifetime of savings, down the drain...

**Optimistic Survivor:** Hey, at least we're still alive! We can always start over!

**(Trailblazer):** Right, that's the spirit! Come on, we'll get through this together!

*(Talk to Yao Guang, optional)*

[Recap] Yao Guang praised Sunday for keeping the affairs inside Perdido Station organized and well-coordinated. Sunday mused that, in times of peril, maybe Order simply meant having someone to step forward and gain recognition during a crisis, and that would be enough.

**Yao Guang:** It's no surprise why you were Penacony's family head, you've organized people, secured supplies, and kept everything running like clockwork.

**Sunday:** This is very much a group effort. Many imagenae friends are assisting. In fact, they know the layout better than we do.

**Yao Guang:** Honestly, this is better than I expected. In a disaster of this magnitude, I thought that panic would overwhelm every aspect of operations.

**Sunday:** Perhaps this "arcadia" isn't as illusory as I once thought... Perhaps (Trailblazer) once showed them how to face a crisis, or perhaps humanity was never so fragile to begin with.

**Sunday:** When their time to be inflicted with real suffering arrived, the people of arcadia knew to hold on to each other.

**Yao Guang:** Yes... unexpected indeed. Even if I can divine fortune and misfortune, I can't predict how people's hearts will change.

**Sunday:** I have often wondered what perfect "Order" should look like. A world of restrictive rules? Peace without any pain?

**Sunday:** But now it seems... in a crisis, all it takes is someone to step forward and say, "This way," and for others to believe and follow... that's enough.

**Yao Guang:** A simple principle, but you made it real. Impressive.

**Sunday:** You flatter me. I must now leave to assess the medicine reserves. Please rest for a while, General.

*(Talk to Kuchiba)*

[Recap] Meowton Station Manager claimed that Perdido Station was well-stocked with supplies, and that they would make the bad guys pay. Meanwhile, Kuchiba pointed out that the excessive concentration of negative Wishpower within Planarcadia was inducing severe stress reactions among the imagenae.

**Meowton Station Manager:** Perdido Station is the largest station in Duomension City. We have adequate supplies, have the refugees head this way!

> **猫条站长：**泊地站可是二维市第一大站，储备物资够用，尽管让难民来这里吧！

**Meowton Station Manager:** And also, if any bad guys try to break in, I'll show them what this kitty's made of!

> **猫条站长：**然后——桀桀桀，要是有坏蛋打进来，就让他们见识见识本喵的厉害！

**Kuchiba:** Oh, (Trailblazer). It's you again.

> **朽叶：**啊，又见面了，开拓者。

- ▸ Station Manager Meowton, what's going on?

> ▸ 猫条站长…怎么了？

- ▸ Why is he so aggressive?

> ▸ 他的攻击性怎么那么强？

**Kuchiba:** The Wishpower generated by the negative emotions in Planarcadia has exceeded a safe level, inducing a stress response in the imagenae.

> **朽叶：**二相乐园负面情绪所催生的愿力浓度已经超过了安全值，诱发了幻造种的应激反应。

**Kuchiba:** Mr. Sunday performed tuning to stabilize the Station Manager's consciousness...But relapses still occur from time to time.

> **朽叶：**星期日先生为站长先生进行了调律，勉强把它的主体意识稳定住了…可时不时还是会复发。

**Meowton Station Manager:** Luckily we have Mr. Sunday! If not for him, I might have done something I'd really regret. He really is...

> **猫条站长：**多亏星期日先生，不然我可能真的会做出无法挽回的事，他可真是……

**Meowton Station (Sullied):** A! No good! Busybody!

> **猫条站长•已黑化：**多！管！闲！事！

**Meowton Station (Sullied):** I'm gonna take those despicable monsters who destroyed my beloved Bullet Wyrm Express, and feed them to Thundercoil!

> **猫条站长•已黑化：**我要把毁掉我最宝贝的疾锋龙列车的那些肮脏怪物们统统送去喂雷虬！

**Meowton Station Manager:** Wh—What's wrong with me...?

> **猫条站长：**呜呜…我这是怎么了？

*(Talk to Kuchiba again, optional)*

**Kuchiba:** The Wishpower generated by the negative emotions in Planarcadia has exceeded a safe level, inducing a stress response in the imagenae.

> **公司安保机甲：**「警告，能量即将耗尽，部分非战斗模块已离线。」

**Kuchiba:** Mr. Sunday performed tuning to stabilize the Station Manager's consciousness...But relapses still occur from time to time.

> **绝望的幸存者：**连它也不行了吗…完了，全完了！

*(Talk to "Narrator")*

[Recap] Ashveil shared an intense, emotional reunion with the "Narrator." As trouble reared its head once more in Ashveil's sanctuary, Ashveil resolutely stepped up to join the fight against Asat Pramad.

**"Narrator":** The detective looked upon his old friend, reunited at last. Through endless near-death escapes, they had always found each other. It should have been ordinary by now.

> **「旁白」：**侦探看着他这位久别重逢的老友。无数次死里逃生，他们总能找到彼此——这本该是习以为常的事。

**Ashveil:** And yet, for some reason, this time, something glimmered quietly in the assistant's sharp, perceptive eyes.

> **不死途：**可不知怎的，这一回，助手那聪慧睿智的双眼中，有什么亮晶晶的东西悄然滚动了一下。

**"Narrator":** It's a pearl, Mr. Ashveil, only a pearl!

> **「旁白」：**是珍珠，不死途先生，是小珍珠啊。

**Ashveil:** Mister N, I am tough as nails. You know that's nothing new. Just a moment ago, some kid told me, "Mr. Ashveil, cut the cringey stuff." I'll pass that along to you too.

> **不死途：**好了，老白，我命硬，你也不是第一天知道啊。就在刚才还有个少年对我说：不死途先生，别整这些尬的。我把这句话也送给你。

**"Narrator":** Heh-heh-heh, welcome back, old friend. But tell me: Why do all your so-called hideaways turn into disasters like this?

> **「旁白」：**嘿嘿，欢迎回来，老伙计。但是怎么你挑的隐居地方回回都会捅出这样的大篓子来？

**Ashveil:** So what does this tell us? It means the trouble always visits the gutsy.

> **不死途：**这说明什么？说明麻烦总爱挑有本事的人找上门。

**"Narrator":** Yes but this time trouble is not only at YOUR door, it's at ALL our doors! And we have nowhere to run to.

> **「旁白」：**但这一回的麻烦可不是上门了，那是堵门，堵住了所有人的门啊。连撤离的地方都没了。

**Ashveil:** Heh, guess that means this troublesome job isn't optional anymore. I've made up my mind, if luck favors us again this time, we're shaking Pearl down for a hefty payout.

> **不死途：**呵，那不就意味着，这单麻烦委托是不接也得接了。我想好了，如果这一回好运再眷顾咱们一次，一定得狠狠敲上真珠一笔。

*(Talk to Narrator again, optional)*

**Ashveil:** Heh, guess that means this troublesome job isn't optional anymore. I've made up my mind, if luck favors us again this time, we're shaking Pearl down for a hefty payout.

*(Talk to Nihilux)*

[Recap] Nihilux and the furbos also fled to Perdido Station for refuge. The furbos already prepared a victory press release for you, and were waiting for you to turn the tide and defeat Asat Pramad.

**Nihilux:** Oh hey, (Trailblazer), what a coincidence. I don't know if I should be happy or sad to meet you here.

> **虚照：**唷，开拓者，好巧好巧。在这里和你相遇，也不知道该庆幸还是该悲伤。

**Nihilux:** I'm known to often leave a note for my editor saying "the author is out searching for inspiration," but this kind of weather wasn't exactly what I had in mind.

> **虚照：**虽然我平时老给编辑留下「作者外出取材中」的告假字条——但是，外出时碰上这样的坏天气，可不在我的取材计划里啊。

- ▸ Did you run away from Inkford Hermitage?

> ▸ 你从渡画泉隐逃出来了？

**(Trailblazer):** Miss Nihilux, are you planning on finishing the script you owe the IPC?

> **开拓者：**虚照老师，你欠公司的稿子不作数了？

**Nihilux:** Urgh! Only a terrifying entity like the editor would focus on deadlines while the world is ending!

> **虚照：**别提！只有编辑这种恐怖的存在才会在世界末日还赶着催稿啊！

- ▸ I knew you'd show up.

> ▸ 我就知道你会出现。

**(Trailblazer):** There's no place I would be surprised to see you.

> **开拓者：**你在哪儿出现，我都不奇怪了。

**Nihilux:** Well, yeah! What if we're entangled together by the threads of destiny?

> **虚照：**是啊，是不是有什么命运的丝线把我们缠在了一起呢？

- ▸ Are the furbos here too?

> ▸ 狸狸们也在这里？

**(Trailblazer):** It's great that you're all safe.

> **开拓者：**还好，你们都还安全。

**Nihilux:** You've seen how fast they run for their lives. They might even be smarter on their feet than I am.

> **虚照：**他们逃命的速度，你也领教过。这些家伙可比我还机灵。

**Paraprince:** Beep boop! My radar is indicating danger everywhere outside. I never thought I'd end up as a combat journalist!

> **幻太子：**哔啵，我的雷达告诉我，外面很危险哩。咱从没想过会有转职成战地记者的一天哩！

**Furbulitzer:** Holy furboly! Explosives... s—so many explosives everywhere! B—But will this poor furbo survive long enough to report on it?

> **普狸策：**社长！您来了！我已经写好您的大胜通稿哩，标题我都想好哩，就叫《绝灭大君归寂突入幻月游戏！星穹列车浣熊力挽狂澜！》

**Furbulitzer:** Boss! You're here! I'm done with your victory press release! I've even thought of a title: "Lord Ravager Asat Pramad Enters The Phantasmoon Games! The Astral Express Attempts To Turn The Tide!"

> **普狸策：**狸了个大谱！素材…到处都是劲爆素材，但、但是本狸有命能发出去吗？

**Furli:** Boss will definitely win!...Right?

> **佛狸：**社长一定会赢的呶！会赢的…吧？

- ▸ I'll win.

> ▸ 会赢的。

- ▸ Nobody gets paid if I lose.

> ▸ 输了就没人给你们发薪了。

**Furbulitzer & Paraprince & Furli:** Boss will win! Boss will win! Boss will win!

> **普狸策&幻太子&佛狸：**社长必胜！社长必胜！社长必胜！

**Nihilux:** Just look, no matter how chaotic things get out there, there's still this little bunch of furbos cheering you on without a second thought. Kinda moving, isn't it?

> **虚照：**看看，即便外面天翻地覆，至少还有这么一群小狸子不问风向地为你加油，是不是很感动啊？

**Nihilux:** You won't let your readers down, will you, (Trailblazer)? I'll include your heroics in the next issue of The Fluffy soon! Stay tuned!

> **虚照：**所以，开拓者，你可别让读者们失望啊——我会把你们的英姿画进漫画里的，届时《绒绒号》将堂堂复更！期待吧！

*(Talk to Nihilux again, optional)*

**Furbulitzer & Paraprince & Furli:** Boss will win! Boss will win! Boss will win!

**Nihilux:** Just look, no matter how chaotic things get out there, there's still this little bunch of furbos cheering you on without a second thought. Kinda moving, isn't it?

**Nihilux:** You won't let your readers down, will you, (Trailblazer)? I'll include your heroics in the next issue of The Fluffy soon! Stay tuned!

*(Talk to the Security Officer)*

**Security Officer:** (Trailblazer), you came at just the right time. Before we lost contact, I received Miss Pearl's pre-issued emergency response plan.

> **治安官：**各位，开拓者，你们来得正好。我在断联之前，收到了真珠小姐提前发布的危机应急预案。

**Security Officer:** We're building an evacuation route around several major subway stations, directing survivors here and to two other safe zones.

> **治安官：**我们正在依托几个主要地铁站建立疏散通道，把幸存者往这里和另外两个安全点集中。

**Security Officer:** Perdido Station is currently the most stable shelter in this area. Thanks to Mr. Sunday and the Express Crew, we managed to organize an early evacuation.

> **治安官：**目前泊地站是这片区域最稳定的避难点了，多亏星期日先生和星穹列车的诸位提前组织了疏散。

- ▸ What's the situation in other areas?

> ▸ 其他地区情况如何？

**Security Officer:** Based on satellite images, Seafeld City has disappeared, literally. Gothum City is shrouded in a fog so thick that any team sent in fails to come back...

> **治安官：**卫星地图上看，海原市几乎整个消失了…不是摧毁，是字面意义的消失。歌坛市那边则被看不见的雾气笼罩，派出的人有去无回……

**Security Officer:** The biggest loss is the spaceship docking bay. Any spaceship that could be used to evacuate is now a total write-off.

> **治安官：**最糟糕的是停泊星船的飞登行所，能向外逃离的飞船全数失灵了。

- ▸ Is it possible to make contact with Pearl from here?

> ▸ 真珠这边能联系上吗？

**Security Officer:** No, all comms are extremely unstable.

> **治安官：**不行，通信状况极其不稳定。

**Security Officer:** Although Madam Pearl activated an emergency response plan and transferred over medical supplies, water purifiers and an isolation barrier generator.

> **治安官：**虽然，真珠女士启动了紧急预案，调来了库存的医疗品、净水器和隔离屏障生成器。

**Security Officer:** But... we're stretched thin on both people and defenses. Those monsters and frenzied imagenae still occasionally attack our defenses.

> **治安官：**但…人手和防御力量严重不足。那些怪物，还有发狂的幻造种，时不时就会冲击防线。

**Security Officer Underling:** Captain, passageway C3 is under heavy pressure. We need support!

**Security Officer:** Send in the second team! Activate the barrier, protect the civilians above all.

**Security Officer:** That's how things are, unfortunately. We try hard to maintain order but the civilians are traumatized, and... well, I'm afraid we can't hold out much longer.

> **治安官：**让二组顶上去！启动屏障，优先保护平民！

*(Talk to the Security Officer again, optional)*

**Security Officer:** That's how things are, unfortunately. We try hard to maintain order but the civilians are traumatized, and... well, I'm afraid we can't hold out much longer.

> **治安官：**抱歉，情况就是这样——我们在尽全力维持秩序，但民众的情绪，恐怕撑不了太久。

### Examine the buried Astral Express

*(Talk to March 7th)*

[Recap] The platform passage was blocked by rubble, and you tried calling out to Pom-Pom, but there was no response. At the same time, Welt and Dan Heng returned to Perdido Station.

The passage is completely blocked by collapsed rubble, every gap sealed shut by twisted rebar.

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), things are really bad.

> **三月七：**开拓者…果然很糟糕。

- ▸ Call out to Pom-Pom loudly.

> ▸ 情况比想象的还要棘手…

**(Trailblazer):** Pom-Pom! Conductor! Can you hear us?

> **开拓者：**帕姆——列车长——听得到吗——

No response.

**March 7th:** Uh-oh...The conductor is probably fine, right?

> **三月七：**糟了…列车长不会被砸晕了吧？

- ▸ Try shifting the rubble.

> ▸ 大声呼唤帕姆。

A deep, ominous groan of stressed metal echoed from within the ruins.

**March 7th:** No way, that's reckless, what if we cause the station to collapse in on itself?

> **三月七：**不行…鲁莽行事，恐怕整个车站都会倒塌的。

- ▸ Things are trickier than I thought...

> ▸ 尝试撬动碎石。

**March 7th:** Let's go back and update the others. We need to come up with a plan together...

> **三月七：**回去把情况告诉大家吧…我们得一起想想办法……

**Sunday:** (Trailblazer), Miss March. Both Mr. Yang and Dan Heng have returned safely!

> **星期日：**开拓者，三月小姐。瓦尔特先生和丹恒平安回来了！

**Blade:** That bird arrived just in time...

> **刃：**那只鸟儿…来得很及时。

**March 7th:** Mr. Yang! Dan Heng! Yay!

> **三月七：**杨叔！丹恒！太好了……

**March 7th:** Himeko is... and the Express... we...

> **三月七：**姬子姐她…还有列车也…我们……

**Welt:** It's alright, March, I already know. We're back now.

> **瓦尔特：**没关系，三月，我知道。我们回来了。

[Recap] You urged the people at Perdido Station to take heart, vowing that the Nameless would triumph over Asat Pramad. Welt had a few ideas about Himeko's clue: "Ignite the Express's engines, win the Phantasmoon Games."

**Welt:** You did well gathering everyone here. I'm sure you ran into your share of trouble along the way.

> **瓦尔特：**能把大家聚集在这里，你们做得很好。这一路上，一定经历了不少坎坷吧？

- ▸ Asat Pramad took Himeko...

> ▸ 姬子被归寂带走了…

**(Trailblazer):** Asat Pramad took Himeko... and I couldn't catch him.

> **开拓者：**归寂带走了姬子…我没能赶上。

- ▸ The arcadia has been totally destroyed...

> ▸ 整个乐园都被毁了…

**(Trailblazer):** There's not a single undamaged block from Graphia Academy all the way to Perdido Station...

> **开拓者：**从绘世学院到泊地站，几乎没有一个完整的街区……

**Dan Heng:** Yes, Blade told us everything on the way here.

> **丹恒：**嗯…一路上刃和我们说了事情的来龙去脉。

**Dan Heng:** We now have an overall picture of the arrival of the apocalypse...

> **丹恒：**关于末日的降临，我们已大致了解——

**Joyful Refugee:** Th—They're the heroes who stopped Fulwish during the livestream!

> **欣喜的避难者：**他们…他们不是直播里阻止满愿的英雄吗！

**Shocked Refugee:** We won't end up buried here!

> **惊讶的避难者：**我们不会被困死在这里了！

**Joyful Refugee:** It's them! We're saved!!

> **欣喜的避难者：**——是他们！太好了，我们有救了！

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** (Trailblazer), perhaps you should say something.

> **星期日：**说些什么吧，开拓者。

**Sunday:** Even if they wake, even if it shatters with a touch, even if I know it to be fake—

> **星期日：**即便终究会醒来，即便一触就碎，即便我无比清醒地知道那是虚假的……

**Sunday:** I would still hope to weave them a dream in this very moment.

> **星期日：**我也希望在这一刻，能为他们编织一个梦。

**Sunday:** Is this me repeating the same mistake? Funny, isn't it.

> **星期日：**这算是重蹈覆辙吗？很奇怪吧。

**(Trailblazer):** This is different, Sunny...

> **开拓者：**不一样，老日……

- ▸ We'll get the Express running again!

> ▸ 我们会重启列车！

**(Trailblazer):** The Astral Express is only stopping here temporarily.

> **开拓者：**星穹列车只是暂时停靠在这里。

**(Trailblazer):** We will repair it, bring everyone onboard, and leave this ruin behind!

> **开拓者：**我们会修好它，载着所有人，离开这片废墟！

- ▸ I'll win the Phantasmoon Games!

> ▸ 我会赢下幻月游戏！

**(Trailblazer):** The wheels of the Astral Express crushed Irontomb. They can also destroy Asat Pramad's canvas...

> **开拓者：**星穹列车的车轮碾过铁墓，也一样能撞破归寂的画布——

**(Trailblazer):** The Nameless will claim victory for all!

> **开拓者：**无名客会为所有人赢下胜利！

**(Trailblazer):** Whether it's a wish, a promise, or a destination...

> **开拓者：**愿望也好，诺言也好，目的地也好。

**(Trailblazer):** As long as everyone believes it can come true, then it is not a dream.

> **开拓者：**只要每个人相信它能够成真…那就不是梦。

**(Trailblazer):** So please, trust me!

> **开拓者：**所以——请相信我吧！

**Impassioned Crowd:** That's great!, we're saved!

> **激昂的人群：**太好了，我们有救了！

**Impassioned Crowd:** Defeat Asat Pramad! Win the Games!

> **激昂的人群：**战胜归寂！赢下游戏！

**Nihilux:** Now that's a fascinating sight...

> **虚照：**可真是个有趣的镜头啊……

**Sunday:** My sincere gratitude, because now, there is not only terror in the Wishpower around us, but a thread of hope as well.

> **星期日：**多谢。弥漫的愿力之中，除了恐惧，终于多了一丝希望。

**Himeko:** The hand holding a tool defines what that tool becomes. In one hand, the scepter triggers a brutal war. In another's hand, the scepter creates new worlds.

> **姬子：**工具的意义在于使用者，就像权杖能发动惨烈的战争，也能催生新世界的萌芽。

**Asat Pramad:** I thought this too. Before my head turned into a die.

> **归寂：**在头还没有变成骰子的时候，我也是这样想的。

**Asat Pramad:** But then Akivili died. The Omen Vanguards began spreading the murmurs of Terminus, and not even Nous could stop the birth of the Destruction.

> **归寂：**但后来，阿基维利死了。厄兆先锋开始散布末王的呢喃，博识尊也没能阻止「毁灭」诞生。

**Asat Pramad:** THEY all failed.

> **归寂：**祂们都是失败者。

**Asat Pramad:** Yet, you are different? I shall wait and see.

> **归寂：**也许你们不一样？我拭目以待。

Procurement List

### Let me see... The "Starblazer"?

> *The design blueprint looks the part, but actually bringing it to life is no easy feat. As a Destruction-flavored footnote to the Trailblaze, though? It certainly fits the bill.*

**Asat Pramad:** The giant reaching for the stars, the myth of Pegana...

> **归寂：**逐星的巨人，裴伽纳的神话……

**Asat Pramad:** She performed a miracle in this world of no imagination.

> **归寂：**在这个失去想象力的世界，她却复现了奇迹。

*(Go to mission target)*

[Recap] Longing to become an individual as free and mighty as a Starblazer, Himeko had sketched out its initial blueprint. Asat Pramad knew nothing about what happened afterward, and Himeko demanded that he hand over control of the imagenesis to her.

**Asat Pramad:** As to how it was created, dear old pal Akivili never spoke a word.

> **归寂：**阿基维利那老伙计，对创造它的方法一直守口如瓶。

**Asat Pramad:** But my daughter replicated the blueprint purely from guesswork. My daughter really is a genius, isn't she?

> **归寂：**难道我的女儿真是个天才？光靠蒙的，就复现了蓝图？

**Himeko:** Would you like to know the secret?

> **姬子：**你想知道其中的秘密？

**Asat Pramad:** No. My dice head wouldn't be able to make heads or tails of such technical details.

> **归寂：**不感兴趣，我的骰子脑袋理解不了这些技术细节。

**Himeko:** What must a girl, powerless but longing for the sky think, to see a giant walking among the stars?

> **姬子：**一个向往天空，却无能为力的女孩，看见能在星间阔步的巨人时，会想到什么？

**Himeko:** She can only draw that dream.

> **姬子：**她只是把自己的梦想画了出来。

**Himeko:** "I want to be as free and powerful as they are."

> **姬子：**「我想成为那样自由、强大的人。」

**Asat Pramad:** So, that isn't a reforged weapon, but a manifestation of Himeko herself?

> **归寂：**所以，那不是被重铸的兵器，而是姬子自我的展现？

**Asat Pramad:** That explains why it is incomplete. Himeko is already dead.

> **归寂：**难怪它不完整。因为姬子已经死了。

**Asat Pramad:** Don't waste your energy. I've seen all your tricks on this journey.

> **归寂：**别白费力气了。这一路上，你的那些小动作我都看在眼里。

**Asat Pramad:** But an incomplete imagenae cannot break this world.

> **归寂：**但残缺的幻造种，打不破这个「世界」。

**Himeko:** You're right. And neither can it be broken by you.

> **姬子：**说得对，打不破——对你来说，也一样。

**Himeko:** Because you know nothing about what follows, and you have no way to imagenate it.

> **姬子：**因为往后的一切，你一无所知，因此也无从幻造。

**Himeko:** How did I start the Express? What miracles did I witness at the end of the Phantasmoon Games? You must allow me to take over if you want to continue on.

> **姬子：**我是如何重启列车的？在幻月游戏的尾声，又见证了怎样的奇迹？想继续前进，你必须把主导权交给我。

**Himeko:** We all know you have no other choice.

> **姬子：**我们都清楚，你别无选择。

**Asat Pramad:** ...Of course, this painting can be altered for you, if you so wish.

> **归寂：**…当然，只要你愿意，这幅画将为你改变。

**Asat Pramad:** It is, after all, the way "you" were born, yes?

> **归寂：**毕竟，那是「你」诞生的始末，不是吗？

*(Investigate the desk, optional)*

(Quick read)

Pegana Starblazing Giant Rebuild Plan

The giant's thrusters, power reactor, and skeletal framework all utilize highly advanced designs, making it fully capable of withstanding the strain of warp jumps and potential impact damage during flight. Its onboard armaments also allow it to serve as an escort, effectively dealing with unknown risks in space. Some see this steel giant manufactured by Pegana as a Trailblaze machine capable of adapting to a variety of extreme environments. I call it... the "Starblazer." The blueprint left behind on the Astral Express is incomplete, making the power reactor and weapon designs difficult to replicate. Even if I managed to replicate them, the vast majority of existing materials wouldn't be able to achieve the Starblazer's designed performance. This led me to a brilliant idea — using imagenesis to replace the original structure. But to bring out its true potential, we still need to restore the missing parts of the blueprint as best we can, and find Wishpower capable of supporting such a behemoth. Can the current Planarcadia really still create such an imagenae? But this is the Astral Express. The Nameless never hesitate over "can it be done." What I should be thinking about is "how to do it." The reconstruction plan is tentatively outlined below: 1. To address the difficulty of restoring the power reactor, it has been iterated into a form that uses Wishpower as an energy source, adapted to the Planarcadia environment. 2. Design a brand-new weapon system for the Starblazer to ensure safety during interstellar travel. Memo: Don't forget to leave extra room for future modifications. ... Crooked text. The memories have grown blurry with time

### Visit "Himeko" in the nursing home

> *The Weathering Curse, a hereditary affliction running through the Graphia family bloodline, has no known cure. As Himeko's condition deteriorated rapidly with age, she had no one to look after her and was forced to check into a sanatorium time and again, each stay only delaying the next episode. So what exactly was it that drove her to accomplish the miracle of fixing the Astral Express?*

[Recap] Shortly after discovering the Astral Express, Himeko's condition deteriorated rapidly, forcing her to be admitted to the sanatorium repeatedly. Sleeping alongside her in the sanatorium was the origin of the connection between Himeko and "Himeko."

**Asat Pramad:** Himeko was lucky. She found the stranded Express and began to repair it.

**Asat Pramad:** Unfortunately, her condition deteriorated rapidly shortly after.

**Asat Pramad:** She was in and out of the sanatorium, but the clock kept ticking down.

**Asat Pramad:** Her stamina worsened by the day. Simply returning to the Express was hard enough, what more reaching the stars?

**Asat Pramad:** She was eventually left with a single choice.

**Asat Pramad:** Your turn to create. Open that window, and fill in the second blank...

**Asat Pramad:** "Your" history with Himeko will also be recreated in this painting.

**Asat Pramad:** I can't wait to see how it turns out.

> **归寂：**我很好奇，那会是怎样的画面？

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

(Wispae dialogues)

Shh—

Don't let the nurse find out.

You're finally here.

*(Complete Interplanar Jump!)*

[Recap] The gravely ill young Himeko made multiple failed attempts to escape the sanatorium, while the nurse urged her to rest properly. On a nearby drawing board sat Himeko's self-portrait, representing the person she could never become.

**Nurse:** Do you know how many times you've failed to escape?

> **护士：**知道这是你第几次逃院未遂吗？

**Young Himeko:** Do you remember how many paintings you produced in class?

> **少女姬子：**你会记得自己在课堂上画过多少幅画吗？

**Nurse:** You know your condition. Take your medicine on time, avoid strenuous exercise.

> **护士：**你的身体状况自己清楚，按时吃药，避免剧烈运动。

**Nurse:** And lie down more often. I'm a Graphia Academy graduate too, I know what it takes to paint.

> **护士：**还有，得多躺着。我也是绘世学院毕业的，知道画画有多辛苦。

**Young Himeko:** You can keep me here, that's fine. But do you also have to meddle with my creative exploits?

> **少女姬子：**不让我走就算了，怎么连创作都要管啊。

**Nurse:** Keep being like this. See if I'll help you next time the doctors catch you.

> **护士：**你再折腾，下回被医生逮到，我可不帮你说话了。

**Nurse:** This painting is so beautiful... Is it your sister? But I remember...

> **护士：**这幅画…好漂亮啊，是你姐姐？但我记得……

**Young Himeko:** No, it's my self-portrait.

> **少女姬子：**不，是我的自画像。

**Young Himeko:** A self I can never become...

> **少女姬子：**…一个我无法成为的自己。

**Nurse:** As long as you're still willing to paint and imagine a better version of yourself, then there's still hope.

> **护士：**…只要你还愿意画，愿意去想象一个更好的自己…那就还有希望。

**Nurse:** I'll be going now. Remember to pick Wednesday for your next escape attempt. I won't be on duty that day.

> **护士：**我先走了，下次逃跑记得选周三，那天我不值班。

[Recap] Taking inspiration from the Navigators in the logs, the young Himeko drew her dream self, infusing her with Wishpower to imagenate her into existence, naming her Motoko. With Motoko's assistance, the repair work on the train progressed smoothly.

**???&Motoko:** I'm back...

> **？？&素子：**…我回来了。

**??:** Still giving the nurse a hard time?

> **？？：**又给护士小姐添麻烦了？

> **少女姬子：**你也要数落我一顿？

**??:** Of course not. Why that face? Did something good happen?

> **？？：**当然不会。看你的表情，是有什么开心的事？

**Young Himeko:** Oh, it's nothing.

> **少女姬子：**没什么。

**Young Himeko:** Looking at this painting reminds me of the day we met...

> **少女姬子：**只是看着这幅画，又想起我们相遇的那天……

**Expectant Girl:** A little wiser and more optimistic, a little prettier.

> **满怀期待的少女：**一点智慧，一点乐观，再稍微漂亮一点。

**Expectant Girl:** Add some color here.

> **满怀期待的少女：**这里再添点颜色。

**Expectant Girl:** And also... Hm, let's see what the Navigators in the logbook are like...

> **满怀期待的少女：**还有，嗯…让我看看日志里的领航员都是什么样的……

**Expectant Girl:** Someone with the courage to fight for what's right, even if they can't gain the upper hand.

> **满怀期待的少女：**即使身处进退存亡，仍应与不义相抗的勇气。

**Expectant Girl:** Someone who's always ready to go ahead, even when there are wheels within wheels.

> **满怀期待的少女：**即使局面纷乱无章，仍应看向前方的应变能力。

> **满怀期待的少女：**然后…最好能再成熟稳重些，像朵莉可或者我见那样。

> **满怀期待的少女：**描摹形体，用自己的记忆和无名客的故事作为骨骼，咳咳……

**Expectant Girl:** And finally, I inject her with Wishpower and materialize her by imagenesis!

> **满怀期待的少女：**最后，注入愿力，幻造成形！

**Self-Portrait:** What a fresh face!

> **自画像：**真是副稚嫩的面孔啊。

**Self-Portrait:** Hello, Creator.

> **自画像：**你好，我的创造者。

> **满怀期待的少女：**成功了，真的活过来了……

> **自画像：**别急，我不会跑掉的。

> **满怀期待的少女：**那个…你好啊？对了，名字！我还没给你起名字呢，对不起！

> **自画像：**不先自我介绍一下吗？

> **少女姬子：**这是，列车的图纸……

**Motoko (?):** Just like we always do.

> **素子（？）：**就和平时一样。

> **少女姬子：**抱歉，让你担心了。

**Motoko (?):** Don't worry about that, nothing wrong with failing.

> **素子（？）：**别有压力，失败了也无所谓。

> **素子（？）：**要这么想：总有人会修好星穹列车，不是非得是我们。但我们的努力，一定能帮到那个人。

> **少女姬子：**可我答应了列车，要带它回家，我不想逃避。

> **素子（？）：**我知道。

> **素子（？）：**所以，撑不住的时候，你更应该想起我。你不是一个人。

> **少女姬子：**差点忘了！等你过来的这段时间，我想到了一种史无前例的咖啡配方，要不要试试？

> **素子（？）：**姬子，在看星星吗？

> **少女姬子：**我在想，列车重启后，我们能去到多远的地方。

**Young Himeko:** The farthest I see is a third-tier star, located ten thousand light-years away. But after that...

> **少女姬子：**我能看见最远的，是一颗一万光年开外的三等星，但再往后……

**Motoko (?):** Past that distance, I will need to use my feet to measure the stars.

> **素子（？）：**再往后的星空，就得用双脚亲自丈量了。

**Young Himeko:** We're running out of time.

> **少女姬子：**留给我们的时间不多了。

**Young Himeko:** Motoko, what's the success rate of igniting the engine?

> **少女姬子：**素子，引擎点火的成功率是多少？

**Motoko (?):** ...

> **素子（？）：**……

**Motoko (?):** "...0.21 percent."

> **素子（？）：**「…0.21%。」

**Young Himeko:** It's gone down but 1 percent...

> **少女姬子：**甚至下降了1%啊……

**Young Himeko:** When I found the Express, it flew me up into the sky. And now, it will not move at all.

> **少女姬子：**明明找到列车的时候，它还带我在天上跑过一程。可现在，却一动不动。

**Young Himeko:** After eliminating hundreds of malfunctions, replacing thousands of parts, here we are, stuck... In the end, it is an engine with zero dynamism.

> **少女姬子：**排除了几百个故障，换掉了上千个零件，最后拦住我们的…却是一台毫无动力学特征的引擎。

**Motoko (?):** Are you okay?

> **素子（？）：**你还好吗？

**Young Himeko:** I'm... alright. It's just the engine. We can handle that.

> **少女姬子：**我…没关系。不就是引擎么？总能搞定的。

**Young Himeko:** I'll think, and you'll do. Just like usual.

> **少女姬子：**我负责想，你负责做，就和平时一样。

**Young Himeko:** It's getting late. You should go get some rest. Even imagenae can't work day and night.

> **少女姬子：**时候不早了，你先回去休息吧。哪怕是幻造种，也不能没日没夜地干活啊。

**Motoko (?):** We will do it, Himeko. We will reach the sky.

> **素子（？）：**姬子，我们一定能飞上天空的。

**Young Himeko:** Sure.

> **少女姬子：**当然。

[Recap] After Motoko left, Himeko grieved alone. Himeko had everything she needed to set out, but the only thing she lacked was time. The moment she passed through the atmosphere, the Weathering Curse would tear her apart.

**Young Himeko:** The engine...

> **少女姬子：**引擎……

**Young Himeko:** Will I really be able to fix the Express?

> **少女姬子：**我真的能修好列车吗？

**Young Himeko:** How far can someone who can barely walk get, even if they leave Ahatopia?

> **少女姬子：**一个连路都走不利索的人，哪怕离开了哈托彼亚，又能走多远？

**Young Himeko:** The Weathering Curse would tear me apart when I cross the atmosphere.

> **少女姬子：**穿过大气层的时候，风化诅咒就会把我撕裂。

**Young Himeko:** I have the Astral Express, I know interstellar travel dynamics, the navigator's log is with me, and I have Motoko. Everything necessary to embark on this journey...

> **少女姬子：**我有星穹列车，有星际航行动力学的知识，有领航员的日志，还有素子。我有启程需要的一切……

**Young Himeko:** Except time.

> **少女姬子：**可唯独没有时间。

**Young Himeko:** I'm sorry, Motoko. Ten thousand light-years is too far.

> **少女姬子：**对不起，素子。一万光年，实在太远了。

**Motoko:** ...

> **素子：**……

### Tell "Himeko" there's another way

> *But there's one thing I still don't understand. Why would Himeko, my daughter, ever enter the Phantasmoon Games? She should've known that she could never gain anything from that ridiculous mask... Other than regret.*

[Recap] Motoko proposed participating in the Phantasmoon Games together with Himeko, using the Starblazer blueprints to build a giant robot as a "miracle." Praying to Aha might ignite the Express's engines.

**Motoko (?):** Himeko, I have an idea.

> **素子（？）：**姬子，我有个主意。

> **素子（？）：**月亮满盈了，我们一起去参加幻月游戏吧。

**Motoko (?):** Pray to The Laughter, the almighty, to grant you a healthy...

> **少女姬子：**没用的，素子。祖辈们早就尝试过了……

> **少女姬子：**他们有些人死在了游戏前夕，有些人在竞赛中落败，只有一位取得过胜利：无量塔弘。

> **少女姬子：**但很遗憾，他失踪了。留下的笔记只写着：「欢愉」无法治愈诅咒。

**Young Himeko:** Thank you, Motoko, but no one can fulfill my wish, not even the Aeons.

**Motoko (?):** ...

> **素子（？）：**……

**Motoko (?):** We'll make a new wish then, not for you to live.

> **素子（？）：**那…就换个愿望，不为生命。

> **素子（？）：**为了大闹一场！让列车再度飞起来，冲向离星星更近的地方。

**Motoko (?):** Let's pray to The Laughter to ignite the engine! And make it so everyone sees the Astral Express set forth anew!

> **素子（？）：**哪怕这家伙一高兴，把列车当烟花点了都行！至少它亮过，响过，飞上天过了！

> **素子（？）：**让他们等着瞧吧。我来当你的手和脚，在所有人面前，把钢铁巨人变出来。

> **素子（？）：**我撬开了你家的保险箱，面具我都带来了。你…不会让我白忙活这一趟吧？

> **少女姬子：**…你说得对，素子。既然风化诅咒无法治愈……

> **少女姬子：**至少，让我用最后的时间，和你一起，把那个「梦」造出来吧。

### So then take "Himeko" and get out of here!

*(Investigate desk, optional)*

**Receptionist (played by Asat Pramad):** This is an unoccupied reception desk. On it is a sparse selection of drinks, ready for patients to help themselves.

> **服务台（归寂饰）：**女士，你想要什么？女士，你听说过马库斯这个名字吗？有种常见的基酒就叫马库斯，当然，那个改变你一生的人…他也叫这个名字。

> **素子（？）：**我想要你的骰子头。

> **书架（归寂饰）：**这本书您一定感兴趣的——《绘世学院杰出校友纪念册》。

> **书架（归寂饰）：**让我来念一下上面的名字吧：美玲、满愿、利安、千世…他们要是活着该有多好啊——

**Motoko (?):** Shut up.

> **素子（？）：**…闭嘴。

[Recap] Asat Pramad mocked this absurd proposal, believing that Himeko had given up her dream of touching the starry sky and her chance of survival just to participate in the Phantasmoon Games, and displayed the ending of the Bloodstained Games.

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** Hahaha... You're even more interesting than I thought. Others are always trying to steal from the gods. But all you want is a brilliant fireworks show.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**哈哈哈…有趣。你比我想得更有意思——他们总想从神手里窃取什么，而你只想献上一场灿烂的烟花。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** And of course, my sweet daughter actually accepted this ridiculous suggestion.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**而我那可爱的女儿，竟然接受了这么荒诞不经的提议。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** Touching, truly! And what did she have to give up to enter the Phantasmoon Games fifteen years ago?

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**多感人啊！为了踏入十五年前那场幻月游戏，她都放弃了什么呀？

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** She gave up the possibility of survival, her dream of touching the stars. And instead, dragged her broken body onwards to share in this madness with you.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**放弃了活下去的可能，放弃了触摸星空的梦，拖着那副快要散架的身体，就为了陪你一起…疯上这么一回。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** We all know how that ended. So please, enjoy this documentary: The Herald of Death's Bloodbath.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**我们都知道结局了。那么，请欣赏纪录片《血战告死魔》——

[Recap] In Asat Pramad's speculation: the Starblazer took shape within Himeko's Wishpower and defeated the Herald of Death. As the winner, Himeko wished to give everything she had to Motoko, remaining in Planarcadia forever while Motoko and the Astral Express soared into the sky.

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** While the crazy Supplicant Marcus Antali was drenching Dovebrook in blood as part of his deranged art, a dying girl and her imagenae friend tried desperately to create a miracle!

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**当走火入魔的疯狂谒者马库斯•安塔利在鸽川鲜血横流的街道上疯狂作画时，一位垂死的少女，和她的幻造朋友倾注自己的全力和心血，创造了奇迹！

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** The Starblazer materialized from your Wishpower, and smashed that frenzied plant into pieces.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**拓星者——它在你们的愿力中成形，击碎了那棵发狂的盆栽。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** As the winner, she made her wish: for Aha to take her real life, her name, her memories, and her dream of Trailblazing the cosmos, and give them all to you.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**作为赢家，她向阿哈许愿：把真实的生命、她的名字、她的记忆…还有那个「开拓银河」的梦，全都送给了你。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** For her part, she would watch you and the Astral Express soar into the sky, while she remained on this planet forever, and with all her regrets, turn to ashes.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**而她自己，在看着你与星穹列车飞向天空时，永远留在了这颗星球的大地上，和所有的遗憾一起，化成了灰。

**Projectionist (played by Asat Pramad):** Come, impostor. Open this door, add the final stroke to the second act of this artwork.

> **放映员（归寂饰）：**来，赝品，打开这扇门，为画作的第二幕添上最后一笔。

[Recap] Before leaving the sanatorium, Himeko felt somewhat afraid, worrying that she would become a burden to Motoko. Motoko urged Himeko to trust her and believe in her future ideal self, joining Himeko on a march to Dovebrook District to halt the Herald of Death's cruelties.

**Young Himeko:** It is completely quiet behind this door...

> **少女姬子：**门外…一点动静也没有。

**Young Himeko:** There's no turning back if we open this door and leave the sanatorium.

> **少女姬子：**打开这扇门，离开疗养院，就没有回头路了。

**Motoko (?):** And we're not planning to return anyway, right?

> **素子（？）：**我们本来也没打算回头，不是吗？

**Young Himeko:** It's just... Can we really win?

> **少女姬子：**可是…真的能赢吗？

**Young Himeko:** And what if I hold you back?

> **少女姬子：**要是我拖了你的后腿，该怎么办？

**Motoko (?):** Then I'll drag you forward.

> **素子（？）：**那我会拖着你走。

**Young Himeko:** ...Don't be ridiculous.

> **少女姬子：**…你在说什么呀。

**Motoko (?):** You're not the one who wished for my birth or to take your place. From the moment I was born, I have only heard you wish to be your own person.

> **素子（？）：**要记住，让我诞生的愿望并非你想要一个人来替代你，从拥有生命的那一刻起，我听到了你的愿望——「想要成为理想中的自己」。

**Motoko (?):** Let me build up the little faith you need: I believe Himeko will go further than anyone else.

> **素子（？）：**而你所缺的这一点小小的「相信」，就让我来为你补上吧——相信姬子会走得比任何人都远。

**Motoko (?):** If you don't believe in yourself in this moment...

> **素子（？）：**如果你不能相信现在的自己……

**Motoko (?):** ...then believe in the future "me."

> **素子（？）：**就相信未来的「我」吧。

**Young Himeko:** ...Thank you. I won't let myself be afraid anymore.

> **少女姬子：**…谢谢。我不会再害怕了。

**Young Himeko:** This battle is not about winners or losers. As we speak, the Herald of Death is freely turning Dovebrook upside down. Someone has to stop him.

> **少女姬子：**这场战斗无关胜利与否…此时此刻，告死魔正在鸽川肆意妄为，必须有人站出来。

**Motoko (?):** ...

> **素子（？）：**……

**Motoko (?):** "When the Phantasmoon is full again, I will be there as I have promised."

> **素子（？）：**「下一次幻月满盈，我一定会赴约的。」

**Motoko (?):** "I will come back with so many stories to tell you."

> **素子（？）：**「带着满满当当的故事，回来讲给你听。」

**Motoko (?):** Let's go then!

> **素子（？）：**那么，走吧！

**Patient (played by Asat Pramad):** Boo-hoo, this is so touching!

**Patient (played by Asat Pramad):** She saved Planarcadia!

**Patient (played by Asat Pramad):** She's a total hero!

**Patient (played by Asat Pramad):** But sadly... They're all dead! Gone! Every last one!

*(Approach the marked location)*

[Recap] Under the starry sky of the sanatorium, Himeko and Motoko reunited within the painting. Motoko believed that over the course of the long journey, Himeko had grown into her ideal self, and she encouraged Himeko to keep moving forward.

**Young Himeko:** There's one thing I still don't understand.

> **少女姬子：**有件事，我还是没想明白。

**Young Himeko:** No matter how many times I painted the Starblazer, I never finished.

> **少女姬子：**以前，我画了那么久拓星者，它一直都不完整。

**Motoko:** I think it may be because uncertainty still plagues you.

> **素子：**我想，是因为你心里还有迷茫。

**Motoko:** Becoming a Navigator, leading the Express across a sea of stars... You haven't seen these things with your own eyes, and you can't imagenate them either.

> **素子：**成为领航员的自己，驾驶列车穿越星海…你没见过这幅景象，也没法幻造出来。

**Young Himeko:** Then how did we defeat the Herald of Death?

> **少女姬子：**那为什么，我们在告死魔面前却成功了？

**Motoko:** Because we have each other.

> **素子：**因为我们在一起。

**Motoko:** And whenever I close my eyes, I see you becoming the Navigator.

> **素子：**而我闭上眼睛，就能看见你成为领航员的样子。

**Motoko:** And the Astral Express readying for departure, its lights illuminating the dark.

> **素子：**星穹列车要启航了，车灯照亮了黑夜。

**Motoko:** Beneath your feet are countless paths, and they are surrounded by nameless stars.

> **素子：**你脚下是数不清的轨道，四周是没有名字的星星。

**Motoko:** You saw a path no one had ever trod before.

> **素子：**你看见了一条路，谁都没走过的路。

**Motoko:** Then you set off on it.

> **素子：**然后，你开始向前。

**Motoko:** Keep moving forward, and the obstacles that once felt impossible to conquer will soon be behind you. And along the way, you'll find companions who are just like you.

> **素子：**只要走下去，你会发现那原本不可逾越的阻碍，成了身后的风景。而在走下去的路上，你会遇到许多和你一样的同伴。

**Motoko:** This is the sight I see every day, and it has never lost its clarity.

> **素子：**这幅景象，我每天都能看见，从来没有模糊过。

**Young Himeko:** I would like to see it too.

> **少女姬子：**我也想看见。

**Motoko & Himeko:** You will.

> **素子&姬子：**你会的。

**Himeko:** How do I look in your vision? Do I look the same?

> **姬子：**我现在的样子，和你想象中一致吗？

**Motoko:** Exactly the same.

> **素子：**分毫不差。

**Himeko:** Fifteen years, Motoko.

> **姬子：**十五年了，素子。

**Himeko:** I never imagined we would reunite in this manner.

> **姬子：**我从没想过，会以这种形式和你重逢。

**Motoko:** Well, it's been fifteen years! You must have walked a long way.

> **素子：**都十五年了啊。你一定走过了很长的路？

**Himeko:** Yes! So long that I learned to repair machines, brew coffee, take care of my juniors, and fight with everything I have...

> **姬子：**是啊。长到我学会了一个人修理机器，冲泡咖啡，照顾后辈，尽力战斗……

**Himeko:** So long that I became like you, and even deceived a Lord Ravager with a talent for disguise.

> **姬子：**长到我变成了你的样子，甚至骗过了一位擅长伪装的绝灭大君。

**Motoko:** It's not time to stop yet. So, will you keep going?

> **素子：**现在不是停下的时候，你还要继续走下去吧？

**Himeko:** Yes.

> **姬子：**嗯。

**Motoko:** Let's go then, Himeko.

> **素子：**那就去吧，姬子。

**Motoko:** Let me be your strength too.

> **素子：**让我也成为你的力量。

[Recap] Fifteen years ago, it was Himeko who survived the Bloodstained Games, not Motoko. Asat Pramad finally understood, noting that the Express's crash lay purely within his choice. Himeko insisted a stranded Express would always restart its journey, and even without her, someone would eventually find and fix the Astral Express.

**Himeko:** Anything you wish to say?

> **姬子：**有什么话想说么？

**Asat Pramad:** What's going on here?

> **归寂：**所以，这是怎么回事？

**Asat Pramad:** Eh? Did I make a mistake? It was Himeko who survived fifteen years ago, not Motoko?

> **归寂：**呃，是我弄错了？十五年前，活下来的不是「素子」，而是「姬子」？

**Himeko:** Hopefully you like this answer... even if it does make you look laughable.

> **姬子：**希望你喜欢这个答案，尽管它会让你显得滑稽可笑。

**Asat Pramad:** No! How can— My daughter, you're alive!?

> **归寂：**不，不——怎么会！你还活着，好女儿！

**Asat Pramad:** This makes me even more interested in you.

> **归寂：**这让我对你更感兴趣了。

**Asat Pramad:** Thousands of years ago, I struck a train down from the same starry sky.

> **归寂：**千年以前，我在同一片星空下将列车击落。

**Asat Pramad:** Fifteen years ago, I sped back toward Planarcadia, and all I could see was a speck of light rising from the horizon.

> **归寂：**十五年前，我匆匆赶回二相乐园，只看见一个光点从地平线上升起。

**Asat Pramad:** I raised my hand and aimed right at it. Just two shots, "bang, bang," and it would fall once more, never to fly again.

> **归寂：**我抬起手，对准它。只要「梆梆」两声，它就会再度坠落，再也无法翱翔。

**Himeko:** But you did no such thing.

> **姬子：**但你没有这么做。

**Asat Pramad:** Indeed. I watched it pass through the atmosphere, leaving a long streak of light in the night sky.

> **归寂：**是啊，我就看着它穿过大气层，在夜空中划过长长的弧线。

**Himeko:** Why?

> **姬子：**为什么？

**Asat Pramad:** Just 'cause. Thought it might be fun.

> **归寂：**我只是觉得，这样很有趣。

**Asat Pramad:** Do you understand? I destroyed the Express once, but I also spared it in a moment of whimsy. Whether the Express is unscathed or destroyed... I decide its fate.

> **归寂：**你明白吗？我曾信手摧毁列车，也曾因一时兴起放任它离去，生存或毁灭，都在我的一念之间。

**Himeko:** No. You're wrong.

> **姬子：**不，你错了。

**Himeko:** I also wondered why I should be the one to find the Express once. What does it really mean?

> **姬子：**我也曾想过，为什么找到列车的人会是我？其中是否蕴含着某种特殊的意义。

**Himeko:** The answer is very simple: the person that found the Express, happened to be me.

> **姬子：**但答案很简单：找到它的人，恰好是我而已。

**Himeko:** The meteor showed the way to the Express from the moment it fell. And for a thousand years, it waited for someone to follow the trail and find it.

> **姬子：**从坠落的那一刻起，流星便指引着列车的方向，它等待了千年，等待有人沿着轨迹抵达它的身边。

**Himeko:** Even if two, three, or even ten thousand years passed, and I was no more, the Express was still going to be found and returned to the sky.

> **姬子：**即便没有我，两千年，三千年，一万年后，也一定会有人找到搁浅的列车，重新启程。

**Himeko:** You're not a god that decides fate. You're just an obsessed egomaniac.

> **姬子：**你不是什么决定命运的神明，你只是个偏执的自大狂。

**Asat Pramad:** Right you are! All gods ARE obsessed egomaniacs!

> **归寂：**说对了，所有神明都是偏执的自大狂！

**Asat Pramad:** My guesses were wrong, but the game isn't over yet. Shall we take another step forward? Fill in the remaining blank space in this painting?

> **归寂：**虽然我的猜想阴差阳错，但游戏还没结束。不妨让我们再往前一步，为这幅画填上最后的空白？

**Asat Pramad:** Round three, question three. Are you ready?

> **归寂：**第三回合，我的第三个问题，你做好准备了吗？

*(Obtain "Gazing into the Deep Blue")*

*(Unlock Achievement The Self-Portrait Called "Trailblaze")*

### Step into the place where the Astral Express set forth anew

> *This imagenesis is drawing to a close, and it looks like we've all had quite the harvest from it, wouldn't you say? That's how it is for me, at least. For this final round, let's lay all our cards on the table.*

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

(Wispae dialogues)

To the farthest star!

Puzzles like this are a piece of cake~

Try summoning a HelpBot~

What happens when Meowton sees HelpBot?

Wisdom is the Nameless's greatest weapon.

Time to leave this place...

You have more important matters to attend to, right?

See you again...

Your name is...

*(Continue to next section of the stage)*

**Asat Pramad:** You thought the Express would bring salvation.

> **归寂：**你以为列车会带来拯救。

*(Complete Shiftmation!)*

**Asat Pramad:** But it will only bring about annihilation once more.

> **归寂：**但它只会再度迎来埃灭。

*(Continue to next section of the stage)*

Don't lose to him.

I'll always be by your side.

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

*(Obtain "Fallen Star's Cradle")*

### Hurry back and finish this painting

*(Approach Asat Pramad)*

[Recap] As for the third secret, Asat Pramad demanded to know the cure for the Weathering Curse. Himeko bared Asat Pramad's true intent: to learn how Aha would react if the Phantasmoon Games broke down. But since Himeko never met with Aha, the answer he wanted did not exist.

**Asat Pramad:** The coast where the Tower of Infinity slumbered, the place from which the Express set off once more...

> **归寂：**「无量塔」沉睡的海岸，列车再度启程之地……

**Asat Pramad:** Or as I frequently call it: Where Repeated Mistakes Begin.

> **归寂：**我更习惯称之为：重蹈覆辙的起点。

**Asat Pramad:** So, how does it feel being back on old stomping grounds?

> **归寂：**故地重游，感觉如何？

**Himeko:** It's just a shame you are my traveling companion.

> **姬子：**只可惜，同行的人是你。

**Asat Pramad:** Better traveling with me, than traveling alone.

> **归寂：**和我同行，总好过孤身一人。

**Asat Pramad:** The Bloodstained Games have no winners. You imagenated your mechatrons, but the remaining Wishpower can't create the audience, much less support a long journey.

> **归寂：**血涂游戏没有胜者。幻造出机铠后，你拥有的愿力甚至不足以完成觐见，更别说启程远行。

**Asat Pramad:** Leading us to the third question: How exactly did you cure the Weathering Curse?

> **归寂：**既然如此，第三个问题：你到底是如何治愈风化诅咒的？

**Himeko:** You're still beating around the bush? Even now?

> **姬子：**事到如今，你还在绕弯子吗？

**Asat Pramad:** Have I ever deviated from the topic at hand?

> **归寂：**我什么时候偏离过正题？

**Himeko:** Three blanks, corresponding to three secrets. They seem to point to me, but they really just conceal your true intentions.

> **姬子：**三处空白，对应三个秘密，看似指向我，实则掩盖了你的真实目的。

**Himeko:** Who left the Starblazer's blueprints on the Express? Who met Himeko and Motoko here? How did THEY cure the irreversible Weathering Curse?

> **姬子：**是谁将拓星者的图纸留在列车？是谁在这里遇见了姬子和素子？祂又做了什么，治愈了不可逆的风化诅咒？

**Himeko:** You have many questions, but none about your so-called "daughter."

> **姬子：**一个女孩的生死，你根本不在乎，即便她是你的「女儿」。

**Himeko:** Your real interest is in Aha.

> **姬子：**你的目标，只有阿哈。

**Asat Pramad:** ...

> **归寂：**……

**Himeko:** The truth from fifteen years ago is key to figuring out: When the Phantasmoon Games are compromised, what form THEY will descend in, and how THEY plan to intervene.

> **姬子：**借助十五年前的真相，你就能弄清：当幻月游戏遭人破坏，祂会以何种面目降临，又会以怎样的态度干涉局势。

**Asat Pramad:** So, I guessed right? THEY appeared and ignited the Astral Express?

> **归寂：**所以，我猜对了？是祂亲自示现，点燃了星穹列车？

**Himeko:** Stop, the game's over then. You set the rules: a secret for a secret.

> **姬子：**停一停吧，游戏结束了。是你定的规则：秘密才能交换秘密。

**Asat Pramad:** You didn't take my trump card.

> **归寂：**你可没有抽走我的底牌。

**Himeko:** Yes, but I took mine.

> **姬子：**对，但我抽走了自己的那张——

**Himeko:** Unfortunately, I wasn't the one who met Aha. The answer you were looking for never existed.

> **姬子：**很可惜，谒见阿哈的人不是我。你想要的答案，从一开始就不存在。

[Recap] Right before vanishing, Motoko pleaded with Himeko to be brave, take the wheel together, and fly the Express into the sky to explore the stars they used to admire.

**Motoko:** That's what THEY said when THEY gave me the gift.

> **素子：**把礼物交给我的时候，祂是这么说的。

"Let's just call it... a long-overdue compensation."

**Young Himeko:** ...

> **少女姬子：**……

**Motoko:** Lost in thought again?

> **素子：**又开始心不在焉了呀。

**Motoko:** You beat the Herald of Death, but you're not happy.

> **素子：**战胜了告死魔，但你并不高兴。

**Young Himeko:** I just... Well, I'm not sure how to put it.

> **少女姬子：**我只是…不知道怎么开口。

**Motoko:** Because I'm leaving soon?

> **素子：**因为我快要走了？

**Motoko:** Once the Express reignites and takes off, there won't be any Wishpower in the vacuum, and imagenae will die out. This is the best outcome.

> **素子：**一旦列车重启飞向天空，真空中没有愿力，幻造种也将不复存在，这是最好的结果。

**Young Himeko:** But...

**Young Himeko:** If I just set off like this, I know it'll haunt my dreams forever.

> **少女姬子：**就这样启程，我以后一定会从梦中惊醒。

**Young Himeko:** Thinking back on it, my dream... was built on someone else's sacrifice.

> **少女姬子：**回忆起今天，我的梦想…建立在另一个人的牺牲上。

**Motoko:** If you feel scared, just look up.

> **素子：**如果感到害怕，就抬头看看吧。

**Motoko:** Up there are the same stars we gazed at together for countless nights.

> **素子：**那里有无数个夜晚，我们一起仰望的星星。

**Motoko:** You'll see all those sights in my stead, and experience all those stories as wonderful and strange... as imagenae.

> **素子：**你会替我见证那些风景，经历那些…像幻造一样奇异的故事。

**Motoko:** You've known all along, haven't you? So be brave, and stop this talk about sacrifices.

> **素子：**你早就明白的，对吧？那就鼓起勇气，别再说什么牺牲了。

**Motoko:** I give my life to conquer the land that trapped us here.

> **素子：**我是用自己的生命，征服了困住我们的大地。

**Young Himeko:** ...

> **少女姬子：**……

**Motoko:** You have to go alone, dummy.

> **素子：**笨蛋，你必须一个人上路了。

**Motoko:** What did you say when you created me? I quote: "If I die, I want to die where the stars are within reach."

> **素子：**在创造我的那一天，是你亲口对我说的吧——「就算死，我也要死在离星空更近的地方」。

**Young Himeko:** When the Phantasmoon is full again, I will be there as I have promised.

> **少女姬子：**下一次幻月满盈，我一定会赴约的。

**Young Himeko:** I will come back with so many stories to tell you.

> **少女姬子：**带着满满当当的故事，回来讲给你听。

**Motoko:** So, deal? If you can't find me to share your stories, then tell them to the moon, like a Mourning Actor.

> **素子：**那就约好了？如果找不着我，也可以像悲悼伶人一样，告诉天上的月亮。

**Motoko:** The moon's shadow said this while giving me my gift.

> **素子：**把礼物交给我的时候，月亮的影子是这么说的。

**Motoko:** "Let's just call it a long-overdue compensation."

> **素子：**「就当是迟来的补偿吧。」

**Motoko:** "It's time we set off once more, Akivili."

> **素子：**「再度启程的时间到了，阿基维利。」

### Reveal Asat Pramad's trump card

[Recap] Himeko saw through Asat Pramad's ability of "possibility imagenesis" and used the gravitational waves generated by the Starblazer's impact to send out a distress signal. Asat Pramad revealed that the actual goal of his trip was neither Himeko nor Aha, but to bring an end to your journey.

**Asat Pramad:** So, it was the "self-portrait" that saw Aha?

> **归寂：**所以，见到阿哈的人是那幅「自画像」？

**Asat Pramad:** You placed a bet with a fake chip?

> **归寂：**你把一枚假筹码当作赌注，放上了牌桌？

**Himeko:** You call it a "challenge," but it's really just a waste of time for you.

> **姬子：**美其名曰「挑战」，但你只是在浪费时间。

**Asat Pramad:** This is a blow, I have to admit. Just a little one, but a blow.

> **归寂：**我得承认，就算只有一点点，我确实受到了打击。

**Asat Pramad:** Though if you don't have the answer, then I can execute Destruction without a shred of guilt.

> **归寂：**可既然答案不在你身上，我也可以毫无负担地…实施「毁灭」了。

**Himeko:** You're wrong, again... This little test was your last chance to find your way back.

> **姬子：**你又错了…这场试探，是你迷途知返的最后机会。

**Asat Pramad:** Interesting. You... were giving me a chance?

> **归寂：**有意思，你…给我机会？

**Himeko:** Even after falling so far as to become a Lord Ravager, I still wished to give the former Navigator a chance to turn back.

> **姬子：**即便堕落成绝灭大君，我依旧想给*前领航员*一个回头的可能。

**Himeko:** But this journey has convinced me: You are not some wayward Nameless. You are just... another enemy trying to dishonor the Express.

> **姬子：**但这一路上，我确信了：你不是什么误入歧途的无名客，你只是一个…让列车蒙羞的敌人。

**Himeko:** And to enemies, I only respond in one way.

> **姬子：**对于敌人，我只有一种回应。

**Asat Pramad:** Destroy me...? Could you really?

> **归寂：**「毁灭」我？你做得到吗？

**Himeko:** I saw right through your tricks, Lord Ravager.

> **姬子：**我看穿了你的戏法，绝灭大君。

**Himeko:** You summoned the apocalypse, and transformed a thousand times, and created scenes from the past to confuse me. But in the end...

> **姬子：**你召来末日，化身万千，又造出过去的景象迷惑我。但说到底——

**Himeko:** You just use reality as a canvas to imagenate the uncertain into truth.

> **姬子：**你只是以现实为画布，将那些不确定的可能性幻造成真。

**Himeko:** You used people's Wishpower to imagenate that impending future born of their fears.

> **姬子：**你用人们的愿力，幻造出了他们恐惧中，那个即将诞生的未来。

**Himeko:** And using the same trick, you imagenated versions of yourself that could exist in different places.

> **姬子：**用同样的手法，你幻造出了「可能存在于其他位置」的自己。

**Himeko:** But when another possibility is observed by others, it crushes the imagenated lie, and your ability loses its effect instantly.

> **姬子：**但是，当另一种可能性被人们观测，压倒幻造的谎言，你的能力也将瞬间失效。

**Asat Pramad:** And what will you do about it, stuck as you are in the World in Canvas?

> **归寂：**那么，被困在画中世界的你，打算怎么做呢？

**Asat Pramad:** Even if you summon the feeble Starblazer, and do your best to crash into the space, you will never escape.

> **归寂：**就算召来残缺的拓星者，拼命撞击空间，你也绝无可能逃出这里。

**Himeko:** Escape? Who said anything about escaping?

> **姬子：**逃？谁告诉你我想逃离？

**Himeko:** Gravity does not only trap people on the ground. It also drags the stars toward each other. Have you, a former Navigator, really forgotten the Nameless distress signal?

> **姬子：**引力不仅能将人们困在地面，也能让星星们奔向彼此。一位前领航员，连无名客的求救手段都忘记了吗？

Starblazer's Collision: Bang, bang bang, bang, bang.

**Himeko:** How they use massive collisions to simulate the gravity wave frequency used by the Express to warp jump? Not even imagenated spaces can block out a signal like that.

> **姬子：**用大质量的撞击模拟列车跃迁时的引力波频率，即使是幻造的空间，也没法阻拦这种特殊信号。

**Himeko:** My companions have known your secret for a while. In a minute or so, you might see your scheme start to fall apart.

> **姬子：**你的秘密，早已暴露在我的同伴面前，也许就在一分钟后，你的野心会付诸东流。

**Himeko:** Or is it seconds, not minutes?

> **姬子：**又或者是…下一秒？

**Asat Pramad:** ...

> **归寂：**……

**Asat Pramad:** Oho! Nicely done. But you have a really terrible habit.

> **归寂：**哈哈哈，干得不错。但你的习惯，实在太糟糕了。

**Asat Pramad:** Does becoming a Navigator mean always facing danger on your own? Well, since this game of ours is ending soon...

> **归寂：**身为领航员，总想着独自面对最凶险的情境？既然你我的游戏已经到了尾声……

**Asat Pramad:** Can you guess how many people I have lined up to play the same game?

> **归寂：**那么猜猜看，我给几个人准备了相同的游戏？

**Asat Pramad:** Go on, you have one chance to guess the answer. The probability of you stopping me is...

> **归寂：**接下来，你有一次机会猜中答案。而阻止我的概率是——

**Asat Pramad:** Zero.

> **归寂：**「零」。

**Himeko:** What exactly are you planning to do?

> **姬子：**你到底…要做什么？

**Asat Pramad:** Who says the great destroyer of Elation came to Planarcadia to kill Aha?

> **归寂：**是谁告诉你，毁灭「欢愉」的大君来到二相乐园，是为了杀死阿哈呢。

**Asat Pramad:** You are not my target... THEY are not my target... Shall I officially announce my plan?

> **归寂：**我的目标不是你，也不是祂。现在，公布答案——

**Asat Pramad:** In the Name of Terminus, (Trailblazer)'s journey ends here.

> **归寂：**以「末王」之名，开拓者的旅途将止步于此。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you won't regret.

## 206　Every Arcadian Corner All At Once　瞬息全乐园

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Desolate Maw's Demonhold　|　角色："Narrator"; Asat Pramad; Ashveil; Dan Heng; Furbulitzer; Furli; Himeko; Magnus; March 7th; Mortenax Blade; Aeon ★ Aha; Paraprince; Pearl; Pom-Pom; Sunday; Trailblazer; Welt; Yao Guang; Thundercoil　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Comedy of Errors'' completed

### Head to the Demon Capital and continue the adventure

> *"Ignite the Express's engines, and win the Phantasmoon Games." Before she was trapped inside the painting, Himeko seemed to have left behind this clue on purpose. Mr. Yang has some thoughts about this. Perhaps we should hear what he has to say.*

[Recap] Using the Express's spatial curvature as an encryption key was a distress method commonly used by the Nameless, and Himeko was hinting for you to use the train's Gravity Probe to receive her signal. While waiting for the walkway to be cleared, Sunday tried to establish contact with Pom-Pom through "tuning."

Some time ago, at Perdido Station...

**Welt:** "Ignite the Express's Engine" is definitely a tad too dramatic if Himeko just wants us to rescue survivors with the Express.

> **瓦尔特：**如果只是启动列车进行救援…姬子恐怕不必强调「点燃列车引擎」。

**March 7th:** What was she implying?

> **三月七：**她是在暗示什么吗？

- ▸ How much could she say if Asat Pramad was with her?

> ▸ 归寂在场…她能说什么？

- ▸ It should be a message that the Nameless can decipher.

> ▸ 应当是无名客才能理解的事…

**(Trailblazer):** If Asat Pramad was close by, then he would have discovered any clues Himeko left behind.

> **开拓者：**归寂当时近在咫尺，姬子留下的任何线索都会被他得知。

**(Trailblazer):** So... her message seems pretty standard, but only we'll know what she really means because we're the Nameless?

> **开拓者：**所以…她暗示我们的，应该是某种听起来平常，但只有我们无名客才明白其意义的事情？

**Dan Heng:** Something about the engine of the Express? Has Himeko mentioned something of this sort in previous journeys? This may be the key.

> **丹恒：**和「列车引擎」有关的事情？在过去的旅途中，姬子提到过什么类似的事吗？也许这会是最关键的提示。

**March 7th:** Well... I kind of zone out whenever Himeko starts talking about warps and calibrating coordinates and all that...

> **三月七：**呜，姬子姐每次谈起跃迁、坐标校准什么的，本姑娘老是左耳进右耳出的……

**March 7th:** Power of Remembrance, activate!

> **三月七：**记忆的力量，快动起来啊！

**March 7th:** A-ha! I remember now. We were back in Penacony...

> **三月七：**啊！我想起来了，是在匹诺康尼——

**March 7th:** Talking about the Charmony Festival, and Himeko said: "The Family sent a music box as an 'Invitation'"... And then she said...

> **三月七：**当时我们谈起谐乐大典，姬子姐说：「家族送来的『邀请函』是一只八音盒」…然后……

**Himeko:** "But the box that was sent to us was slightly unusual... The music it played had off-key notes in the background."

> **姬子：**「可送抵我们手中的这只却有些奇怪…它播放的乐音背景中掺杂了一些不和谐音。」

**Himeko:** "I recorded these dissonant notes, and they were a perfect match with the gravity wave frequency when the Express made its warp jump. Also, the Express engine's spatial curvature was its encryption key."

> **姬子：**「我转录下这些杂音，它们与列车跃迁时的引力波频率完全吻合，而以列车引擎的空间曲率为密钥——我得到了上面这段话。」

**Welt:** "...A distress call method regularly used by the Nameless."

> **瓦尔特：**「…是无名客常用的求救手段。」

- ▸ Gravity Wave frequency!

> ▸ 引力波频率！

- ▸ The Express engine's spatial curvature is its encryption key!

> ▸ 列车引擎的曲率是密钥！

**(Trailblazer):** I remember now! Pearl said something similar not too long ago...

> **开拓者：**我也想起来了！就在不久前，真珠也说过类似的事——

**Pearl:** "A conflict broke out between Miss Himeko and an entity suspected to be Asat Pramad... a gravitational wave effect caused by a giant mass was observed..."

> **真珠：**「姬子女士与疑似归寂的存在爆发了冲突…观测到巨大质量导致的引力波效应。」

**Welt:** Himeko is hinting that we should use the Gravity Probe on the Express to receive her signal!

> **瓦尔特：**所以，姬子是在暗示我们利用列车上的引力探针接收她发来的信号！

**March 7th:** That's exactly it!

> **三月七：**这下全对上啦！

**Welt:** If that's the case, we need to get ready as soon as possible!

> **瓦尔特：**既然如此，我们必须尽快做好准备！

**Dan Heng:** It'll take some time to clear the passage to the Express. Sunday, do you have a way to contact the conductor?

> **丹恒：**清理通道还需要些时间，星期日，你有办法直接联络到列车长吗？

**Sunday:** The distance does not pose a problem for tuning. However, my birds must first lock onto the conductor as their target.

**Sunday:** I will do my best. Please excuse me for a moment.

> **星期日：**单论距离，调律不成问题，但我的鸟儿必须先锁定列车长作为目标。

*(Unlock Achievement The Gravity Wave That Never Fades)*

### Check in on how everyone else is doing

> *Mystery solved! Himeko's sending us a distress signal via gravitational waves! But to decode the information, we'll need to use the Gravity Probe on the Express. While Sunday's searching for Pom-Pom, let's check in on how everyone else is doing.*

*(Idle)*

**Sunday:** Let me open my senses...

> **星期日：**请给我些时间，我会尽力一试。

*(Talk to Ashveil, optional)*

**"Narrator":** The detective's eyes take in everything. His face is inscrutable, though the rate of his respiration is tellingly point three seconds above normal. By all indications, he is uneasy.

> **「旁白」：**侦探的目光在四周逡巡，他脸上没什么表情，但呼吸的节奏比平时快了零点三秒——这意味着，他在担忧。

**Ashveil:** Uneasy? Who?

> **不死途：**担忧？哪有的事。

**"Narrator":** The source of his unease is the little raccoon's imminent departure on a mortal journey, and the survivors of the disaster that befell this city.

> **「旁白」：**他在担忧那个即将踏上险途的小浣熊，也在担忧这座城市里每一个尚未被灾难吞噬的幸存者。

**Ashveil:** Alright, Mr. N, that'll do. You seem to have time on your hands. Help me clear this passage.

> **不死途：**停，老白，停。有这工夫不妨来给我搭把手挖掘通道。

**"Narrator":** Nine hundred credits an hour plus two bananas does not cover this task, Mr. Ashveil.

> **「旁白」：**九百信用点加两根香蕉的时薪不包干这个的，不死途先生。

**Ashveil:** Shut your mouth, then. Go on patrol. As a detective's assistant, your job is to keep an eye on everything when the detective is otherwise occupied.

> **不死途：**…那就别话多。去巡逻吧，作为助手，在侦探忙前忙后的时候，帮我看好场子吧。

*(Talk to Mortenax Blade)*

[Recap] Blade received word that Silver Wolf had already escorted Graphia to Pearluxe Tower. Silver Wolf and Pearl were currently restoring Planarcadia's communications and, with Graphia's assistance, attempting to encanvas Planarcadia once again.

**Blade:** I received a message that Silver Wolf has arrived at Pearluxe Tower.

> **刃：**我收到了银狼抵达珠星大厦的消息。

**Blade:** Together with Pearl, she'll repair the communication systems across Planarcadia.

> **刃：**接下来，她会和真珠一起修复二相乐园的全域通信。

**Blade:** Then, with Graphia's assistance, they'll attempt to re-enact the ritual from a millennium ago, folding all of Planarcadia back into the canvas — the most extreme measure of protection.

> **刃：**在绘世的协助下，她们将尝试重启千年前的仪式，将整个二相乐园再次折叠入画中——这是最极端的保护手段。

**Blade:** How's the situation here?

> **刃：**你这边的情况如何？

> ▸ 有进展了！

**Blade:** Very good.

> **刃：**很好。

> ▸ 在担心银狼吗？

**Blade:** No. I trust her.

> **刃：**不，我相信她。

### Use Sunday's tuning to contact Pom-Pom

> *Mystery solved! Himeko's sending us a distress signal via gravitational waves! But to decode the information, we'll need to use the Gravity Probe on the Express. Sunday performed tuning to track down Pom-Pom, which means we can report the situation to the conductor.*

**Sunday:** Good news, everyone! The conductor is safe and sound.

> **星期日：**诸位，好消息，列车长安然无恙！

*(Talk to Sunday)*

[Recap] Pom-Pom managed to decode the intelligence relayed by Himeko, revealing that Asat Pramad was a Lord Ravager capable of observing possibilities and manifesting them through imagenesis. Overturning this doomsday required defeating Asat Pramad before the eyes of the entire world. Yao Guang suggested invoking the Reignbow Spiritus "Kālacakra Skyplume" while you, aided by Sunday's tuning, stood atop the television tower to observe Asat Pramad in Yao Guang's stead, pinpointing his true form to forge an opening for victory.

**Sunday:** My bird has located them. I am able to share what the conductor is seeing.

> **星期日：**我的鸟儿找到了列车长，我现在已经能共享它的视线了。

**Sunday:** A moment, please, while I inform them of the situation...

> **星期日：**稍等，我这就向它说明情况——

A few moments later...

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Dan Heng:** Any progress?

> **丹恒：**有进展了吗？

**Sunday:** Yes. The conductor says they have locked on to a weak gravitational wave, and are decoding it...

> **星期日：**嗯。列车长说，它锁定了一道微弱的引力波信号，并且正在解码——

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom sure is glad that everybody's okay!

> **帕姆：**大家都没事，太好了帕！

**March 7th:** Conductor! We missed you too!

> **三月七：**列车长！我们也好想你呀！

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom received Himeko's message! Repeating it now...

> **帕姆：**姬子的消息，我已收到帕！这就向各位乘客转述——

**Pom-Pom:** "The source of Asat Pramad's power is fear. He exploits people's fears to imagenate future possibilities."

> **帕姆：**「归寂利用人们的恐惧，幻造未来的可能，这就是他的能力。」

**Pom-Pom:** "When another possibility is observed, this apocalypse will be overwritten."

> **帕姆：**「当观测到另一种可能性，这个末日将会被覆盖。」

**Pom-Pom:** And! And also...

> **帕姆：**还有！还有一句话——

**Pom-Pom:** "Each and every one of us Nameless is making it to the next stop!" This part is the conductor's heartfelt wish!

> **帕姆：**「无名客们，一个都不许少，我们还要出发去下一站帕！」这也是列车长的心声帕！

- ▸ We're coming, Himeko!

> ▸ 姬子…等着我们！

- ▸ We'll do our best, Conductor!

> ▸ 列车长，我们会加油的！

**Welt:** So, Asat Pramad is a Lord Ravager that observes and imagenates possibilities.

> **瓦尔特：**…所以，归寂是一位观测可能性，并加以幻造的绝灭大君。

**Dan Heng:** That explains why I could never land a blow on him.

> **丹恒：**难怪和他交手时，攻击总会落空。

**Welt:** When people saw the Lord Ravager's descent, they were gripped with fear, despair, and everything else that comes with an apocalyptic end. Those very emotions fed this catastrophe and made a possibility become reality.

> **瓦尔特：**也正因为人们目击了绝灭大君的降临，恐惧、绝望、种种「末日到来」的想象笼罩内心，所以这片末日废墟才拥有了存在的可能。

**March 7th:** Himeko said we need to observe another possibility for this apocalypse to be overwritten. "Observe another possibility"... How do we do that?

> **三月七：**姬子姐说，要改变这个末日，就要观测到另一种可能…怎么才算「观测到另一种可能性」？

**Yao Guang:** Build a crowd, then defeat Asat Pramad in front of them. That's the easiest way.

> **爻光：**最简单的法子，当然是在众目睽睽下击败归寂。

**Yao Guang:** But he'll just create hundreds of clones to hide behind, and no one will be able to lay a finger on him. That's another of his tricks by manipulating possibilities.

> **爻光：**但，要命中他已是难上加难，他还鱼目混珠造就了无数分身，隐藏了本体——这也是他操纵可能性的戏法吧。

**March 7th:** So, we don't actually have any way of defeating that old dicehead!

> **三月七：**那…岂不是完全没办法打倒那个坏老骰了！

**Yao Guang:** Who says we're out of options?

> **爻光：**倒也…并非无路可走。

- ▸ General, what ideas are you cooking?

> ▸ 将军有主意了？

**Yao Guang:** If we somehow witness all his possibilities, then he would collapse into just one being due to the effect of observation.

> **爻光：**如果有办法将归寂所有的可能性尽收眼底，便能通过观测，迫使他的存在坍缩归一。

**Yao Guang:** I will activate the formation plume I've set up all over the arcadia, then use the vision of the Reignbow Spiritus "Kālacakra Skyplume"!

> **爻光：**我将激活此前布设在乐园各处的阵羽，请降帝弓威灵「时轮天雉明王」开眼！

**Sunday:** General, do you require my cooperation like during SparxiCon?

> **星期日：**将军，需要我像「火花大会」时那样配合吗？

**Yao Guang:** "...Each reading drives the murky future toward a definite outcome, a collapsed quantum state, even if that outcome isn't to one's liking."

> **爻光：**「…每一次测算，都会将混沌不清的未来推向某个确定的坍缩结果，即便这结果并不为人所乐见。」

**Yao Guang:** "...The 'Eye of Īśvara' would transcend mere sight and permeate throughout this realm. The Kālacakra Skyplume would predict the future of all beings at tens of billions of instances per second."

> **爻光：**「…『观自在眼』将超脱目光所限，遍入此界，时轮天雉明王会以每秒百亿次计判读众生的未来。」

**Yao Guang:** That's right, Mr. Sunday. I would like you to expand the range of your "tuning"...

> **爻光：**不错，星期日先生，我想请你全力扩张「调律」的范围——

**Sunday:** You mean to determine the future of Asat Pramad through observation? But... as I recall, as your observation expands, it will anchor the fate of this world.

> **星期日：**您要以观测锚定归寂的未来？但…我记得，随着您的观测扩张，它也会锚定这个世界的命运。

**Yao Guang:** Observation is for (Trailblazer) to worry about.

> **爻光：**观测，这是开拓者要操心的事了。

- ▸ Me? You're leaving the observation to me?

> ▸ 我？…我来观测吗？

- ▸ What should I do, then?

> ▸ 那我该怎么做呢？

**Yao Guang:** I want you to become "my eyes" and look for me.

> **爻光：**我要你成为「我的眼」，代我窥看。

**Yao Guang:** The fate Asat Pramad imagenated is imminent, and there is no path for the future in it. Only "īśvara," only observers with sufficient strength and personal freedom have what it takes to make choices that trace the paths to our future.

> **爻光：**归寂幻造的凶命迫在眉睫，未来已无路可走。唯有「自在之人」——唯有足够强大自由的观测者才能做出选择，为我们擘画通往未来的路。

**Yao Guang:** I also need a place with a good vantage point... Something like a towering structure.

> **爻光：**我还需要一个视野足够开阔的地方，比如…一座高塔。

**Welt:** How confident are you in this plan succeeding, General?

> **瓦尔特：**将军这个计划…有几成把握？

**Yao Guang:** ...

> **爻光：**……

**Yao Guang:** Even if the chance of success is only a coin toss, once I enter the fray myself... I always treat it as a "one-hundred-percent certainty."

> **爻光：**哪怕成事机会仅有「半成」，在我以身入局时，我总会把它看作「十成十」。

- ▸ I accept.

> ▸ 我接受。

- ▸ I believe in you.

> ▸ 我信你。

**(Trailblazer):** I'll fight by your side too, even if there is only half a chance of success.

> **开拓者：**哪怕机会只有半成，我也会和你一起战斗。

**Sunday:** I will do all I can to ensure you two remain connected through "tuning."

> **星期日：**我会尽我所能，以「调律」连接两位。

**Welt:** Then, Dan Heng, March, and I will clear the passages as soon as possible, to ensure the Express can depart.

> **瓦尔特：**那么，我、丹恒、小三月会尽快清空坍塌的隧道，帮助列车脱困。

**Sunday:** And myself, (Trailblazer), and General Yao Guang will force the collapse of Asat Pramad's clones, granting us a way to his true form.

> **星期日：**而我、开拓者和爻光将军会坍缩归寂的分身，锁定他的本体。

**Welt:** Thank you, Sunday. Before you boarded the Express, you said that you didn't understand Trailblazing. And yet, you have fought alongside us in the most dangerous moments of our journey.

> **瓦尔特：**谢谢你，星期日。登上列车前，你曾说自己「尚未理解开拓」，但这一路的旅行中你总会在最危险的时刻和我们一起战斗。

**Sunday:** No, Mr. Yang, I should be the one thanking all of you. Before boarding the Express, I was just a traveler looking for answers.

> **星期日：**不，瓦尔特先生，是我要谢谢各位才对。在登上列车前，我只是一位寻找答案的旅人。

**Sunday:** You all taught me to Trailblaze and walk the stars. To hold fast to the hands of those around me when facing difficulties. It is my honor to be able to provide assistance.

> **星期日：**是你们教会了我「开拓」不仅要触碰远方的星星，也要握紧身边人的手跨越地上的坎坷——能成为各位的助力，是我的荣幸。

**Welt:** You really look like one of the Nameless now.

> **瓦尔特：**你已经很有无名客的样子了。

**Dan Heng:** Thank you too, General Yao Guang. The Express will meet you as soon as we rescue Himeko!

> **丹恒：**爻光将军，也谢谢你。一旦救出姬子，列车会尽快赶来和你们会合！

**March 7th:** And that's when we'll defeat Asat Pramad together!

> **三月七：**到时候，咱们一起打倒归寂！

- ▸ Let's go. Himeko is waiting.

> ▸ 动身吧，姬子还在等着。

- ▸ Let's bring her home.

> ▸ 该去带她回家了。

**(Trailblazer):** And when we do bring Himeko home, each and every one of us will be accounted for as well.

> **开拓者：**嗯，带姬子回家的时候，我们一个都不能少。

**Yao Guang:** (Trailblazer), you still have time. Go and say your goodbyes to your friends before we set off. Meet me at the entrance when you're ready.

> **爻光：**开拓者，在出发前，你还有一些时间和那些熟悉的人告别，等准备好了，就去入口见我。

### Talk to Yao Guang and prepare to head out

> *According to the intel Himeko sent back, the only way to defeat Asat Pramad is to force the possibilities of its existence to collapse. The sole method available right now is to ascend to higher ground and use Yao Guang's "Eye of Īśvara" to lock onto Asat Pramad. This also means Yao Guang and I will be exposed in a highly vulnerable position, so we'll need to exercise extreme caution. Before setting out, perhaps I should take the time to say a proper farewell to my companions.*

*(Talk to Ashveil, optional)*

**"Narrator":** The young man/woman's eyes burn with fiery determination. For the path he/she is to traverse soon, leads straight into the eye of the storm.

> **「旁白」：**少年/少女的眼中，此刻燃烧着烈焰般的决心…他/她正踏上一条，通往风暴中心的道路。

**Ashveil:** Off to save the world again, Nameless? Be sure to return in one piece.

> **不死途：**又要出发去拯救世界了吗，无名客？要安全回来啊。

- ▸ Aren't you coming along, Ranger?

> ▸ 我就把这话当祝福吧。

**Ashveil:** I'm staying to help your friends dig out the Express.

> **不死途：**我会帮你的朋友们一起将列车掘出来。

> ▸ 你也一样。

**Ashveil:** Don't you worry. Not even a rat will be able to find a way in here. You focus on destroying that dice-headed scum's lair.

> **不死途：**放心，一只老鼠也别想溜进来。你只管去掀了那骰子混蛋的老窝。

**Ashveil:** Go on. I'll take care of everything here.

> **不死途：**放心去吧，交给我就行。

*(Talk to Mortenax Blade, optional)*

**Blade:** I knew you were leaving soon. Take care. Don't lose your life.

- ▸ I'll take that as a blessing.

> ▸ 不一起吗，游侠？

- ▸ You too.

> ▸ 这里就交给你了。

**(Trailblazer):** Wait, no! That doesn't sound... Just forget it!

**(Trailblazer):** I'm off. See you later.

*(Talk to Nihilux, optional)*

**Nihilux:** Hm? Why do you look like that? What's happening? Is it bad...?

> **虚照：**哦？看这表情…是要出发去办大事了？

**Nihilux:** The little furbos and I will be here waiting to hear the story of how you claimed victory over Asat Pramad. Don't forget!

> **虚照：**要记得哟，我和小狸子们会在这里等你回来讲你如何脚踩归寂大赢特赢的传奇故事。

**Paraprince:** Boss! You absolutely must win! W—We'll be waiting to shoot your victory cover!

> **幻太子：**社长…一定要赢哩！咱、咱还等着给您拍凯旋的封面哩！

**Furbulitzer:** I've already sent out the press release! Just waiting to fill in the time and location of the victory!

> **普狸策：**通稿我已经先出了哩！只差最后填上胜利时间和地点哩！

**Furli:** Winner, winner! Boss is definitely winning!

> **佛狸：**会赢的呶！社长一定会赢的！

*(Talk to Yao Guang)*

[Recap] Before departure, Yao Guang handed you a strategy pouch. Yao Guang asked you to observe her as much as possible to prevent her from disappearing due to "Temporal Dislocation," even if it meant anchoring her ill fate.

**Yao Guang:** Are you ready?

> **爻光：**准备好了？

**Yao Guang:** Before we set off, there's something I'd like you to keep safe for me, (Trailblazer). Please hold on to my strategy pouch.

> **爻光：**在出发前，我有样东西要托付给你。开拓者，请收下这个锦囊。

- ▸ What's inside?

> ▸ 里面封着什么？

**Yao Guang:** Our future destinies.

> **爻光：**我们未来的命运。

- ▸ When should I open it?

> ▸ 我该在什么时候打开？

**Yao Guang:** Open it when you are in need of the Hunt.

> **爻光：**在你需要「巡猎」的时刻，你会打开它的。

**Yao Guang:** Remember when we were at SparxiCon? And you saw the Weal and Woe Lots hanging over me?

> **爻光：**还记得在火花大会时，你曾目睹过我头顶悬着吉凶签的事么？

- ▸ I remember.

> ▸ 记得。

**(Trailblazer):** I remember. But you commanded me not to tell you what they said.

> **开拓者：**我记得，但按你的吩咐，我不能把结果告诉你。

- ▸ I don't remember.

> ▸ 不记得了。

**(Trailblazer):** There's been so much going on lately... I don't really remember.

> **开拓者：**这阵子发生了太多事，我有些记不清了。

- ▸ I'll restrain my curiosity.

> ▸ 我会克制好奇的。

**(Trailblazer):** I remember. I'll do my best not to glance at your head.

> **开拓者：**我记得，我会尽我所能不看向你头顶的。

**Yao Guang:** No, I want the opposite this time.

**Yao Guang:** I'd like you to keep your eyes on me as much as possible.

> **爻光：**不，这一次，我的要求恰恰相反。

- ▸ Why?

> ▸ 为什么？

**Yao Guang:** Thirty years ago, I used the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier to divine the tides of battle for the Fanghu. But in a moment of blind arrogance, I turned my back on what destiny had revealed, and dared to wield the Reignbow Spiritus to twist the very threads of causality.

**Yao Guang:** But karma was on the way to punish me. That day, I became entangled with the barrier at a single point, left to drift endlessly through time and space. A healer named this unusual condition "Temporal Dislocation."

> **爻光：**三十年前，我以「十方光映法界」占算方壶战局，却因一念倨傲，罔顾天命昭示，妄图用帝弓威灵篡改因果。

**Yao Guang:** Put simply, there's always a chance that I could suddenly disappear at any moment. If I am not observed for even a second, I may disappear.

> **爻光：**报应来势汹汹——那一日，我和阵法纠缠于一点，开始在时空中摆荡不休。有医士将这一奇症命名为「共时错位」。

- ▸ Is there no way to reverse it?

> ▸ …没办法恢复吗？

**Yao Guang:** I restrain myself with the Nexus Sapientiae forged by the Yuque Artisanship Commission. Sometimes it works, sometimes it doesn't.

> **归寂：**可没想到，来的是一位卜者。我还以为能有幸会一会令幻胧饮恨的神策将军呢。她又能为你做些什么？计算胜率？预卜吉凶？

**Asat Pramad:** Whatever she divines, I will block its path. What will you do now?

> **归寂：**无论她如何占算，所有的道路都已被我封死。你们又该如何走下一步？

> **爻光：**就让我这从不现身人前的卜者教你一课吧——

> **爻光：**归寂，将军与卜者的职责截然不同：将军破阵于后，卜者，破阵于先！

> **爻光：**你说封死「所有道路」？抱歉，「开拓」最擅长在没有道路的地方踏出前路。
