> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Desolate Maw's Demonhold　|　角色：Trailblazer; Asat Pramad; Himeko; Himeko • Nova; Motoko; Starblazer; March 7th; Evernight; Dan Heng; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae; Dan Heng • Imbibitor Lunae; Welt; Sunday; Pom-Pom; Misha; Mikhail; Kafka; Silver Wolf; Asta; Natasha; Bronya; Seele; Cocolia; Clara; Svarog; Acheron; Firefly; Tribbie; Trianne; Trinnon; Aglaea; Castorice; Cipher; Hyacine; Mydei; Hysilens; Cerydra; Phainon; Anaxa; Nanook; Aeon ★ Aha; Cyrene; Jing Yuan; Fu Xuan; Mem; Pearl; The Other Trailblazer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Every Arcadian Corner All At Once'' completed

### Ask Asat Pramad your questions

> *Welcome, (Trailblazer), Supplicant of Phantasmoon Games, and protagonist of the Astral Express. Our shared journey along the Path of Trailblaze has earned us this conversation. Please accept this gift, a token of care from a former Navigator to the next generation. Enjoy the thrill of rolling the dice... especially when it decides just how much of the truth you'll uncover.*

[Recap] Are you really planning to skip your conversation with me? You should at least show some respect to a senior in the Trailblaze journey! It won't be too late to make your decision after you take a look at the questions I've prepared for this quiz game.

**(Trailblazer):** ...The Phantasmoon Courtyard?

> **开拓者：**…幻月秘庭？

**Asat Pramad:** A terrifying enemy is near, but you stand alone, helpless. How often have you been in such a predicament... Protagonist?

> **归寂：**可怕的敌人近在咫尺，自己却孤立无援。像这样的绝境，这一路来你遇到过多少次…「主角」？

- ▸ I'm used to it.
- ▸ Every single time.

**Asat Pramad:** This is how trailblazing expeditions always end, no? Don't worry... as the former navigator myself, I may provide you with some instruction.

> **归寂：**开拓之旅总会变成这样，不是吗？别紧张，作为前领航员，我也许可以给你一些指点。

**Asat Pramad:** Just as Akivili instructed me, and I instructed Falcon Amundsen.

> **归寂：**就像阿基维利指点过我，而我也指点过法尔肯•阿蒙森那样。

**Asat Pramad:** Hmm, perhaps "instruction" is too formal a word. Young people these days can't stand such formality.

> **归寂：**啊不不不，说「指点」未免有些登味太重，现在年轻人都不怎么喜欢。

**Asat Pramad:** How about we make it a bit friendlier, you quiz me instead?

> **归寂：**要不，换个更亲切的方式吧：你来考考我？

**Asat Pramad:** Next, you can ask me questions... Eight of them...

> **归寂：**接下来，你可以向我提问…八个问题——

**Asat Pramad:** About me, about Aha, about Akivili, about the future of the cosmos, about you even... I'll answer anything you ask.

> **归寂：**关于我，关于阿哈，关于阿基维利，关于银河的未来…甚至关于你自己。有问有答，我来者不拒。

- ▸ Are you really that kind?
- ▸ Why do I have to ask you questions?

**Asat Pramad:** Because once eight questions are done, you're dead.

> **归寂：**因为八个问题后，我会杀死你。

**Asat Pramad:** Did you forget? We are the only two Supplicants with a chance of winning the Phantasmoon Games!

> **归寂：**你忘了吗？我们可是这场幻月游戏中唯二有望胜利的谒者啊！

- ▸ Why eight questions?

**Asat Pramad:** Because the die happened to land on eight...because there are eight Supplicants in the Games.

> **归寂：**因为随手掷出的骰子结果为八…因为幻月游戏的谒者一共八人。

**Asat Pramad:** Or perhaps because we first met in Amphoreus... another "8"! Hahaha!

> **归寂：**又或者，因为你我的初次相逢是在翁法罗斯——那又是一个「8」，哈哈。

- ▸ I'm not interested in playing a dumb trivia game.

**Asat Pramad:** You're not interested in stories and secrets? That's unfortunate.

> **归寂：**对故事和秘密不感兴趣？真是遗憾。

**Asat Pramad:** Of course, this universe also has a button reserved for you.

> **归寂：**当然，这片宇宙也为你预留了一枚按钮。归寂指向剧情跳过按钮

**Asat Pramad:** But let me remind you, this is the last time we will speak so openly.

> **归寂：**但容我提醒你，这样坦诚的对话机会可不会有第二次了。

- ▸ (Think about Asat Pramad's offer)

**(Trailblazer):** (Is this guy actually serious?)

> **开拓者：**（这家伙…是认真的？）

**(Trailblazer):** (Himeko hasn't escaped yet, and there's no news from the Express. I should try to delay him for as long as possible if we want to have any chance of winning.)

> **开拓者：**（姬子还没脱困，列车也没有消息，我应该尽可能拖延时间，争取胜利的机会。）

**(Trailblazer):** Why would I believe a Lord Ravager?

> **开拓者：**我…为什么要相信一个绝灭大君？

**Asat Pramad:** Hm. Looks like you're not so opposed to my offer either.

**Asat Pramad:** How about we add a wildcard to this boring trivia game then: Give your answers to the die you're holding.

> **归寂：**哈，看来你也不是很抗拒这个提议嘛。

- ▸ Die?
- ▸ What game are you playing?

**Asat Pramad:** Roll the die. If your roll is a critical success, you get to uncover my deepest secret. I will answer every question, even if it works against me.

> **归寂：**那么——让我们为无聊的问答游戏增添一点变数：把答案交给你手中的骰子如何？

- ▸ And if the roll succeeds but isn't a critical success?

**Asat Pramad:** I'll share a candid truth... though it won't necessarily be the whole truth.

> **归寂：**抛出这枚骰子——如果运气爆棚，掷出了大成功，你会撬开我藏得最深的秘密。我会告诉你答案，毫无保留——哪怕它不利于我。

- ▸ And if the roll is bad?

**Asat Pramad:** I still answer, but only in half-truths. It shouldn't pose any problem for our protagonist, you can effortlessly distinguish truth from deception.

> **归寂：**我依然会回答，但答案将披上半真半假的谎言，我们聪明的主角当然能一眼分辨哪些是真的，哪些是假的。

- ▸ How do I know you'll play fairly?

**Asat Pramad:** Didn't General Yao Guang bind me just now? Hahaha! You should really trust her strength!

> **归寂：**就在刚才，那位爻光将军不是牢牢地把我捆住了吗？哈哈，你要相信她的实力啊。

**Asat Pramad:** Besides, it's been a long time since I've just offered myself up to the chessboard of fate.

> **归寂：**而且，我也很久很久没有毫无防备地把自己抛在命运的棋盘上了。

**Asat Pramad:** How exciting to have every outcome based on pure probability!

> **归寂：**由纯粹的概率牵引结果，能有这样一个机会，真是再刺激不过了！

**Asat Pramad:** And now, let's get started!

> **归寂：**那么，让我们开始吧！

[Recap] It appears my sentiments were entirely unreciprocated. Let the secrets sleep forever within the uncast dice. Given that you willingly surrender all claims to the truth and harbor no further inquiries, I shall waste no more breath upon you.

**(Trailblazer):** (What do I ask?)

> **开拓者：**（该问什么问题呢？）

**Asat Pramad:** Right now, among infinite possibilities, countless versions of you are asking me endless questions.

> **归寂：**此时此刻，在无穷个可能中，数不清的你正在向我提出无数问题。

**Asat Pramad:** Not strange at all, faced with fate and the universe, people have endless questions. It's asking and answering that make us who we are.

> **归寂：**不奇怪，面对命运和宇宙，人有无限的问题，正是提问和回答造就了我们。

**Asat Pramad:** Don't hold back! Go ahead and ask your most pressing question right now.

> **归寂：**不必犹豫，问出你此刻最想问的那个问题就好。

- ▸ Why do you call me Akivili?

**(Trailblazer):** Why were so many people calling me "Akivili" along the way?

> **开拓者：**为什么这一路的旅程中，有许多人称呼我「阿基维利」？

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** How lucky. Although the question you asked is difficult to answer.

> **归寂：**运气不错。虽然你问出了一个我难以回答的问题。

**Asat Pramad:** They call you Akivili, because not only are you the successor, but at the core level, you are becoming the focus of the Path of the Trailblaze.

> **归寂：**他们称呼你为阿基维利，因为你不仅仅是继承者——在某种更本质的层面上，你正在成为「开拓」命途的焦点——

**Asat Pramad:** This isn't imitation. It's the Path itself responding, naturally, to the absence of its god. You are not THEM yet, but you are headed in that direction.

> **归寂：**这并非模仿，而是命途本身在缺失神明后自然而然的反应。你不是祂，但你正在走向那个位置。

**Asat Pramad:** Even more important is the thing inside you...

> **归寂：**更重要的是…你身体里的那个东西。

**(Trailblazer):** A Stellaron?

> **开拓者：**星核？

(If the player already asked about the Stellaron)

**Asat Pramad:** Correct. I've already answered your question about it.

> **归寂：**你想知道更多？那就是另一个问题了。

(If the player hasn't asked about the Stellaron)

**Asat Pramad:** Do you want to know more? That would be a different question.

> **归寂：**没错，我刚刚已经回答过你关于它的问题了。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Because the path you walk is the same one THEY once walked.

> **归寂：**因为你走的路，正是祂曾走过的路。

**Asat Pramad:** The Astral Express, the Path of Trailblaze, your determination to connect the worlds... None of these are coincidences.

> **归寂：**星穹列车，开拓的命途，连接世界的决心…这些并非巧合。

**Asat Pramad:** You are a successor in a way, carrying an essence similar to THEIRS, and so people see a familiar starlight in you. This is the reason, I think, that Himeko thinks so highly of you.

> **归寂：**你是某种继承者，承载着与祂相似的本质。人们在你身上看到了熟悉的星光，我想，姬子也是因此对你如此高看。

**Asat Pramad:** Congratulations, your roll came up short.

> **归寂：**恭喜你，运气检定失败了！

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** "Why do so many people call me 'Akivili'?" How narcissistic of you.

> **归寂：**「为什么许多人称呼我『阿基维利』？」你可真够自恋的。

**Asat Pramad:** They call you Akivili simply because people habitually project lost expectations onto others. You are a mirror that reflects their wishes.

> **归寂：**他们称你为「阿基维利」不为别的，只是因为人习惯于把失去的期待投射在别人身上罢了。你，是一面映照他们愿望的镜子。

**Asat Pramad:** Let me say something that might sting. You know, back when I served as the Astral Express navigator, some people called me the "Second Coming of Akivili."

> **归寂：**说些伤人的事情。你知道吗，在我担任列车领航员的时代里，也有人称呼我为「阿基维利再世」。

- ▸ Why do you want to kill Aha?

**(Trailblazer):** As the Lord Ravager aiming to "destroy Elation," what are you planning to do to Aha?

> **开拓者：**作为「毁灭欢愉」的绝灭大君…你想对阿哈做什么？

(If the player already asked about the Fourth Finality)

**Asat Pramad:** When you asked about the Fourth Finality, I already answered you.

**Asat Pramad:** Before THEY fully succumb to corruption and drag all existence into that unspeakable madness... I will grant THEM the grandest final curtain.

> **归寂：**刚才你问到「第四终末」时，我已经回答过你了。

**Asat Pramad:** Never mind, I won't count that one. Go ahead, ask another question.

> **归寂：**算了，这个问题我不会把它计入次数的，你再选一个提问吧。

(Move on to the next question without reducing the number of questions left)

**Asat Pramad:** Ha! What else would the Lord Ravager aiming to destroy Elation do? Destroy THEM, of course.

> **归寂：**哈，毁灭「欢愉」的绝灭大君…还能做什么？当然是毁掉祂咯。

**(Trailblazer):** I meant what exactly is your hatred for THEM about?

> **开拓者：**我是说，你到底和祂有什么深仇大恨？

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Well, I may have developed some excessive feelings towards Aha. After all, before boarding the Express, the Fool's name I used at the Tavern was... "Enroute."

> **归寂：**对阿哈，我也许产生了一丝多余的情感。毕竟，在登上星穹列车之前，我在「酒馆」中使用的愚者之名…唤作「未抵」。

**Asat Pramad:** Hmph! You're pretty lucky. You think I'm going to kill Aha? I've already tried.

> **归寂：**哼，运气不错。你以为我要杀死阿哈？我早试过了。

**Asat Pramad:** Everything I do is to save THEM.

> **归寂：**我的所做所为，是在拯救祂。

**Asat Pramad:** Once, Elation was a dutiful and committed god. THEY scattered the seeds of chance, carving sharp turns of fate across the universe. THEY even made a reward of THEIR own face, bestowing it upon those who passed THEIR trials, and delighted in goading other gods to join the hunt for the most terrifying, supreme beings...

> **归寂：**过去，「欢愉」曾是一位尽心尽责的神明：祂播撒偶然，为宇宙带去刀锋般转折的命运；祂会将自己的面相当作奖赏，赐予通过挑战的生灵；祂还热爱撺掇众神一同围猎至高的恐怖存在——

**Asat Pramad:** Unfortunately, nothing resists the incineration of time. No matter how long the comedy lasts, all will eventually return to silence.

> **归寂：**可惜，没有什么东西能抵抗时间的焚烧。无论欢笑多久，一切终会归于死寂。

**Asat Pramad:** But before the deathly silence, the Elation will become something even more terrifying.

> **归寂：**但在死寂到来之前，「欢愉」会化作某种更可怕的东西。

**Asat Pramad:** I saw how truly fatigued THEY are. The Elation is being swallowed by THEIR own endless mirth.

> **归寂：**我看见了祂真实的疲惫——「欢愉」正在被自己无尽的「愉悦」所吞噬。

**Asat Pramad:** THEY will slide towards an even more terrifying extreme: not to stop elation, but to drag all things into an eternal, meaningless, and unreasonable farce of chaos. And that will cease to be comedy, and become a universe-wide contamination.

> **归寂：**祂将滑向一个更可怕的极端：不是停止欢愉，而是将万物都拖入一场没有意义、没有逻辑的永恒混沌闹剧。那不再是喜剧，而是宇宙级别的污染。

- ▸ The Elation will fall...?

**Asat Pramad:** Upon stepping onto a Path and becoming an Aeon, one is expected to glimpse THEIR own end, yet the results of Paths colliding remain beyond THEIR sight.

> **归寂：**在踏上命途的时刻，成为星神的存在理当对自己的结局有所预见——尽管祂并不能看到命途交汇碰撞的结果。

**Asat Pramad:** How do THEY really feel about exercising the divine authority of Elation... Do THEY suffer with it? Would THEY fear this ending? I don't know.

> **归寂：**祂到底是怀着怎样的心情，行使「欢愉」的神权…祂会痛苦吗？祂会畏惧这结局吗？我不知道。

**Asat Pramad:** For humans, the prize of these games lasts for only a fleeting minute. But for an Aeon, it's a free and blissful reprieve from the shackles of THEIR Path.

> **归寂：**这场游戏的奖赏，对人类来说是稍纵即逝的一分钟；但对星神而言，却是逃脱命途重负，逍遥自在的假释期。

**Asat Pramad:** If the disciples of a god cannot bring THEM deliverance before THEY fall, do they still deserve to be called disciples?

> **归寂：**如果一位神明的信徒不能在祂堕落前为祂带来解脱，又怎么配称得上信徒呢？

**Asat Pramad:** I boarded the Astral Express and traveled to different worlds, searching for THEIR tracks and games...

> **归寂：**我搭上了星穹列车，前往不同的世界，搜索着祂的足迹和游戏——

**Asat Pramad:** Before THEY fully succumb to corruption and drag all existence into that unspeakable madness... I will grant THEM the grandest final curtain.

> **归寂：**我要在祂彻底腐化、将一切存在拉入那不可名状的疯狂之前…为祂送上最盛大的谢幕。

**Asat Pramad:** This is the highest form of reverence I can offer THEM.

> **归寂：**这是我对祂最大的敬意。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Once, Elation was a dutiful and committed god. THEY scattered the seeds of chance, carving sharp turns of fate across the universe. THEY even made a reward of THEIR own face, bestowing it upon those who passed THEIR trials, and delighted in goading other gods to join the hunt for the most terrifying, supreme beings...

> **归寂：**过去，「欢愉」曾是一位尽心尽责的神明：祂播撒偶然，为宇宙带去刀锋般转折的命运；祂会将自己的面相当作奖赏，赐予通过挑战的生灵；祂还热爱撺掇众神一同围猎至高的恐怖存在——

**Asat Pramad:** Unfortunately, nothing resists the incineration of time. No matter how long the comedy lasts, all will eventually return to silence.

> **归寂：**可惜，没有什么东西能抵抗时间的焚烧。无论欢笑多久，一切终会归于死寂。

**Asat Pramad:** But before the deathly silence, the Elation will become something even more terrifying.

> **归寂：**但在死寂到来之前，「欢愉」会化作某种更可怕的东西。

**Asat Pramad:** And that's why I have made up my mind to kill THEM.

> **归寂：**所以，我决定杀死祂。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Oh dear, luck isn't on your side. So whatever I plan to do to Aha... you'll never know.

> **归寂：**哎呀，运气不站你这边。所以，我想对阿哈做什么，你无从得知。

**Asat Pramad:** In any case, I am not in Planarcadia for Aha.

> **归寂：**何况，这一次抵达二相乐园的我…也不为阿哈而来。

**Asat Pramad:** I came here for you.

> **归寂：**我是为你而来的。

- ▸ How can I defeat you?

**(Trailblazer):** I'm sure it's nothing to you, but do you mind explaining how to defeat you?

> **开拓者：**你应该不会介意回答我，该如何击败你吧？

**Asat Pramad:** Understandable. I've met a lot of people who love coming at me yelling their slogans: "Hope!" "Justice!"

> **归寂：**而你，是喜欢查完攻略打魔王的那一款。

**Asat Pramad:** But you would rather know all the strategies to beat the devil.

> **归寂：**放心，我的答案不会让你失望的。当然，前提是你要投出骰子。

**Asat Pramad:** Don't worry... my answer won't disappoint you. But of course, you'll have to roll the die first.

> **归寂：**哇哦，竟然投出了大成功！

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** My, oh my! So I, in my capacity as senior and devil both, must now tell you, "protagonist," how to defeat me. How thrilling!

> **归寂：**好家伙！身兼前辈与魔王两重职责的我，竟然要把击败自己的方法亲口告诉「主角」，真是个激动人心的场景啊。

**Asat Pramad:** I won't hide it anymore, then. The way to defeat me is right in front of you, inside you, in fact, hidden away.

> **归寂：**那么，我就不藏着掖着了。打败我的方法近在眼前：它就藏在你的身体里。

- ▸ A Stellaron?

**Asat Pramad:** Bingo! You now have the means to kill me.

> **归寂：**回答正确！你本就拥有能杀死我的手段。

**Asat Pramad:** But unfortunately, your limited, laughable, human form cannot defeat me. You're trapped within a lie called the "Trailblaze," dreaming you are a courageous human.

> **归寂：**但很可惜，你有限的、可笑的人类形体根本无法击败我——你被困在了一个名为「开拓」的谎言里，做梦以为自己是个勇敢的人类。

**Asat Pramad:** But if you do what you did against Fulwish, open it to this world... to those cowards trembling in the ruins, to the audience waiting for a hero to save them, the surge of Wishpower will fully awaken it!

> **归寂：**但只要你像对付满愿时那样，向这个世界…向那些躲在废墟里瑟瑟发抖的懦夫，向那些指望着勇者拯救世界的观众们敞开它，涌来的愿力将会彻底激活它！

- ▸ What will happen to me then?
- ▸ What will the Stellaron turn me into...?

**Asat Pramad:** What does it matter? You would have defeated the devil, and your wish would be fulfilled, no?

> **归寂：**管他呢…打败魔王，你的愿望已经完成了，不是吗？

**Asat Pramad:** And when that time comes, you may no longer care about the cost.

> **归寂：**至于那个时候的你，也许不会在乎这份代价了。

**(Trailblazer):** ...

> **开拓者：**……

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** The way to defeat me is right in front of you: inside you, in fact— it's the Stellaron.

> **归寂：**打败我的方法近在眼前：它就藏在你的身体里——星核。

**Asat Pramad:** All this time, you've been traveling in a limited and ridiculous human form. You're trapped within a lie called the "Trailblaze," dreaming you are a courageous human. You can't defeat me in this state.

> **归寂：**一直以来，你都在用有限的、可笑的人类形体踏足于旅途。你被困在了一个名为「开拓」的谎言里，做梦以为自己是个勇敢的人类。你不可能以这个模样击败我。

**Asat Pramad:** But if you do what you did against Fulwish, open it to this world... to those cowards trembling in the ruins, to the audience waiting for a hero to save them, the surge of Wishpower will fully awaken it!

> **归寂：**但只要你像对付满愿时那样，向这个世界…向那些躲在废墟里瑟瑟发抖的懦夫，向那些指望着勇者拯救世界的观众们敞开它，涌来的愿力将会彻底激活它！

**Asat Pramad:** And in that moment, you can easily seize your victory.

> **归寂：**那一刻，你可以轻松将胜利握在手中。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Curses! Your failed roll stings more for me than it does for you.

> **归寂：**可恶。相信我，你投出失败，我比你还难受。

**Asat Pramad:** What an opportunity to miss out on. Well then, let me give you a little hint.

> **归寂：**多有趣的机会，就这么错过了。那么，我给你一个小小的提示吧。

**Asat Pramad:** You unknowingly found a way to defeat me when you were fighting back against Fulwish.

> **归寂：**在你对抗满愿的时候，你已无意中找到了击败我的方法。

**Asat Pramad:** Try your hardest to remember. You already know what I'm talking about.

> **归寂：**好好回忆回忆，你知道我在说什么。

- ▸ What is the Fourth Finality?

**(Trailblazer):** Someone said there are four Paths that will bring the apocalypse to the universe: Destruction, Harmony, and Nihility...

> **开拓者：**有人告诉我，有四条命途将为宇宙带去末日：毁灭、同谐、虚无……

**(Trailblazer):** What is the last remaining Path?

> **开拓者：**剩下的最后一条命途，是什么？

**Asat Pramad:** Your questions would leave even Nous completely stumped. But I will answer them, even though I can only offer my humble opinion. Up to you if you believe me or not. Now, roll the die!

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Hmm... perhaps I have a surprise for you. It's an answer I realized over the course of a long journey...

> **归寂：**啧啧，不妨让我给你一个惊喜，这是我在漫长旅程中领悟的答案——

**Asat Pramad:** The fourth path that brings the apocalypse is the Trailblaze.

> **归寂：**第四条带来末日的命途，是「开拓」。

- ▸ Liar!
- ▸ The Trailblaze? That's not possible...

**Asat Pramad:** You should be glad that THEIR plan has failed. Nobody knows what happens to a universe that has its Paths broken.

> **归寂：**你们应该庆幸祂的计划失败了。没有人知晓一个被打破命途的宇宙会变成什么样。

**Asat Pramad:** An unknowable, incalculable universe of madness... a universe where every direction is forward, and there is no end. Time flows, yet nothing ever happens. Space stretches on, yet there is nowhere to go... even existence itself defies all logic.

> **归寂：**一个无法演算，不可估量的疯狂宇宙… 一个所有方向都是前方，永远没有终点的宇宙。时间流逝却无事发生，空间延展却无处可去…连存在本身都毫无逻辑。

**Asat Pramad:** Thinking about it, doesn't even the answer "Destruction" sound kind? At least it has a clear end...

> **归寂：**这么一想，是不是连「毁灭」这个答案，听起来都显得仁慈了？至少…它还有个明确的句点。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** I believe the fourth Path that brings the apocalypse is the Elation.

> **归寂：**我认为，第四条带来末日的命途，是「欢愉」。

- ▸ The Elation...?
- ▸ How is that possible?

(If the player already asked about killing Aha)

**Asat Pramad:** I've already given you the reason.

> **归寂：**理由，我刚才已经告诉过你了。

**Asat Pramad:** The carnival that plunges everything into chaos, turning everything into a meaningless farce... this mania would be an unbearable burden for the universe.

> **归寂：**令一切坠入混沌的狂欢，令一切成为无意义的闹剧——这份「疯狂」将是寰宇无法承负的重荷。

(Otherwise)

**Asat Pramad:** Once, Elation was a dutiful and committed god. THEY scattered the seeds of chance, carving sharp turns of fate across the universe. THEY even made a reward of THEIR own face, bestowing it upon those who passed THEIR trials, and delighted in goading other gods to join the hunt for the most terrifying, supreme beings...

> **归寂：**过去，「欢愉」曾是一位尽心尽责的神明：祂播撒偶然，为宇宙带去刀锋般转折的命运；祂会将自己的面相当作奖赏，赐予通过挑战的生灵；祂还热爱撺掇众神一同围猎至高的恐怖存在——

**Asat Pramad:** Unfortunately, nothing resists the incineration of time. No matter how long the comedy lasts, all will eventually return to silence.

> **归寂：**可惜，没有什么东西能抵抗时间的焚烧，无论欢笑多久，一切终会归于死寂。

**Asat Pramad:** But before the deathly silence, the Elation will become something even more terrifying.

> **归寂：**但在死寂到来之前，「欢愉」会化作某种更可怕的东西。

**Asat Pramad:** I saw how truly fatigued THEY are. The Elation is being swallowed by THEIR own endless mirth.

> **归寂：**我看见了祂真实的疲惫——「欢愉」正在被自己无尽的「愉悦」所吞噬。

**Asat Pramad:** THEY will slide towards an even more terrifying extreme: not to stop elation, but to drag all things into an eternal, meaningless, and unreasonable farce of chaos. And that will cease to be comedy, and become a universe-wide contamination.

> **归寂：**祂将滑向一个更可怕的极端：不是停止欢愉，而是将万物都拖入一场没有意义、没有逻辑的永恒混沌闹剧。那不再是喜剧，而是宇宙级别的污染。

- ▸ The Elation will fall...?

**Asat Pramad:** Upon stepping onto a Path and becoming an Aeon, one is expected to glimpse THEIR own end, yet the results of Paths colliding remain beyond THEIR sight.

> **归寂：**在踏上命途的时刻，成为星神的存在理当对自己的结局有所预见——尽管祂并不能看到命途交汇碰撞的结果。

**Asat Pramad:** How do THEY really feel about exercising the divine authority of Elation... Do THEY suffer with it? Would THEY fear this ending? I don't know.

> **归寂：**祂到底是怀着怎样的心情，行使「欢愉」的神权…祂会痛苦吗？祂会畏惧这结局吗？我不知道。

**Asat Pramad:** If the disciples of a god cannot bring THEM deliverance before THEY fall, do they still deserve to be called disciples?

> **归寂：**如果一位神明的信徒不能在祂堕落前为祂带来解脱，又怎么配称得上信徒呢？

**Asat Pramad:** So, I boarded the Astral Express and traveled to different worlds, searching for THEIR tracks and games...

> **归寂：**所以，我搭上了星穹列车，前往不同的世界，搜索着祂的足迹和游戏——

**Asat Pramad:** Before THEY fully succumb to corruption and drag all existence into that unspeakable madness... I will grant THEM the grandest final curtain.

> **归寂：**我要在祂彻底腐化、将一切存在拉入那不可名状的疯狂之前…为祂送上最盛大的谢幕。

**Asat Pramad:** This is the highest form of reverence I can offer THEM.

> **归寂：**这是我对祂最大的敬意。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** You're not rolling well, but that won't stop me from telling you my opinion.

> **归寂：**掷骰的结果不乐观，但不妨碍我会告诉你我的看法。

**Asat Pramad:** The fourth path that will bring about the apocalypse is the Preservation.

> **归寂：**第四条将带来末日的命途，是「存护」。

- ▸ No big surprise there...
- ▸ Is that logical?

**Asat Pramad:** Of course it doesn't sound logical if the purpose of the Preservation is to sustain the existence of the universe itself. So THEY probably went a step too far...

> **归寂：**当然不合理。如果「存护」的职责是为了维系宇宙本身的存在…那么祂显然做过了头。

**Asat Pramad:** No, it shouldn't be THEY, but all of THEM rather. All the Gods who created and sustained the Paths of the universe... They define the boundaries, deciding which will become reality, and which will be erased.

> **归寂：**不，不应该说祂，而是祂们，所有创造和维持命途宇宙的神明们——祂们划定边界、决定何者成真，何者当被抹消。

**Asat Pramad:** And within that grand order woven by the gods, a paradox had long been planted, an unforeseeable ending, one even Nous, the omniscient god failed to foresee.

> **归寂：**而在那由众神共同编织的恢弘秩序中，早已埋下了连博识尊也无法预见的悖论——全知之神无法预见自己不能预见的结局。

**Asat Pramad:** When every variable is fixed by the Path, the unseen, and forever unseeable "unknown" begins to grow quietly at the edge of all rules, fated to become the end.

> **归寂：**当每一个变量都被命途「固定」，那未被看见、也永远无法被看见的「未知」，就会在所有规则的尽头悄然滋生，注定成为末日。

**Asat Pramad:** So, what brought the fourth finality was not a specific Path, but all Paths themselves. It was the gods THEMSELVES who, in the name of creation and existence, carved out an inescapable finale with their own hands.

> **归寂：**所以，带来第四终末的，并非某个具体的命途，而是所有命途本身。是神明们以「创造」与「存在」为名，为自己亲手划定了无路可逃的终局。

- ▸ About the other "me"

**(Trailblazer):** I once met my other self in Amphoreus. Who was that?

> **开拓者：**在翁法罗斯，我曾见到另一个自己。那个人是谁？

**Asat Pramad:** ...Oh? This is... a most interesting discovery. Might you also possess the gift of grasping "possibility"?

> **归寂：**…嗯？这可真是…令人惊喜的发现。莫非你也有触及「可能性」的天赋？

**Asat Pramad:** Unfortunately, even I do not know the answer to that question. It's beyond what I can see and understand at present.

> **归寂：**遗憾的是，这个问题的答案，连我也未曾窥见。 它超出了我此刻能观测与理解的范畴。

**Asat Pramad:** To make amends, this question... won't count against you.

> **归寂：**作为补偿，这次提问…就当是免费赠送的。

- ▸ Are Yao Guang and Evanescia okay?

**(Trailblazer):** I want to know how Yao Guang and Evanescia are doing.

> **开拓者：**我想知道…爻光和绯英现在怎么样了？

**Asat Pramad:** I can tell you care a lot about them.

> **归寂：**看得出来，你很关心她们。

**Asat Pramad:** As you should, hehe. After all, they both bet everything on you.

> **归寂：**呵呵，也是，毕竟她们两人毫无保留地将希望的赌注押在了你的身上。

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Ah. This roll buys you two answers in one go.

> **归寂：**哇哦，竟然投出了大成功！

**Asat Pramad:** The Xianzhou general used her "gaze" to anchor the fate and future of this world, paving the way for her comrades. What a stirring, bittersweet tale. Isn't that exactly what the Xianzhou people admire?

> **归寂：**唔，既然掷出了这个结果，那么我会一次性解答你的两个疑惑。

**Asat Pramad:** But during her observation, the only thing she could not take into account was... herself.

> **归寂：**那位仙舟将军以自己的「目光」锚定这个世界的命运与未来，为战友铺平道路，真是可歌可泣。他们仙舟人不就喜欢这个调调吗？

**Asat Pramad:** So I offered a small gesture in return: I brought the probability of her existence at this moment... down to virtually zero.

> **归寂：**不过，当她向外观测的时刻，唯一无法顾及的，就是作为观测者的自身。

**Asat Pramad:** Is she dead or alive? I do not know, and it makes no difference. She will be lost forever... lost in a maze of possibilities.

> **归寂：**所以我也给了一点小小的回敬：让她存在于此刻的概率无限趋近于…零。

**Asat Pramad:** She might find her way home... Maybe tomorrow, or maybe tens of thousands of years from now.

> **归寂：**她还活着吗？我不知道。因为她活着和死了已经毫无区别了。她将永远迷路…迷失在可能性的迷宫里。

**Asat Pramad:** As for Evanescia... She is the avatar of the abundant Elwood. You don't need to worry about her safety.

> **归寂：**她也许能找到回来的路…但可能是明天，也可能是在几万年之后。

**Asat Pramad:** What you should worry about, is whether she will still be the Evanescia you know when she wakes up again.

> **归寂：**至于绯英…作为丰饶神木的化身，你用不着担心她的安危。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** But, as is customary, I can only answer one question at a time. Who do you pick?

> **归寂：**你更应该担心的是——当她再度苏醒，还究竟是不是你认识的那个她。

- ▸ Yao Guang

**Asat Pramad:** How should one define this Xianzhou general's state of existence?

> **归寂：**但是，按照惯例，我一次只回答一个问题。做个选择吧？

**Asat Pramad:** She's still alive, but she may as well be dead, because she will be lost in the maze of possibilities... forever.

> **归寂：**该如何定义这位仙舟将军的存在状态呢？

- ▸ Evanescia.

**Asat Pramad:** She is the avatar of the abundant Elwood. Even if the world ended, she would not be affected.

> **归寂：**她还活着，不过与死了毫无区别。因为她将永远迷路…迷失在可能性的迷宫里。

**Asat Pramad:** What you should worry about, is whether she will still be the Evanescia you know when she wakes up again.

> **归寂：**她可是丰饶神木的化身，就算世界毁灭也轮不到她。

**Asat Pramad:** Do you really have the time to worry about others? You should be thinking hard about what you're going to do next.

> **归寂：**你更应该担心的是——当她再度苏醒，还究竟是不是你认识的那个她。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Oh my, what a poor roll. Just assume they're both dead.

> **归寂：**哦哟，骰子结果很不妙。你就当她们都死了吧。

**Asat Pramad:** Do you really have the time to worry about others? You should be thinking hard about what you're going to do next.

> **归寂：**我说，你还有闲工夫操心别人？好好想想你自己接下来要做什么吧。

- ▸ Hot take on the Lord Ravager

**(Trailblazer):** Since you're open to answering anything, let's talk about the Lord Ravagers' weaknesses!

> **开拓者：**既然你什么都不介意回答，那说说绝灭大君的弱点吧！

**Asat Pramad:** The Lord Ravagers' weaknesses? How greedy, child. You just asked seven questions at once.

> **归寂：**——但是，谁又能拒绝一个嚼同事舌根的好机会呢？

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** But then again, who could pass up the chance to gossip about colleagues?

> **归寂：**更别说，你还投出了大成功！说吧，想听谁的弱点？

**Asat Pramad:** And plus, your roll was a critical success! Go ahead then: Whose weakness do you want to know?

> **归寂：**不，干脆买一送七，我给你包圆了吧。

**Asat Pramad:** No, scratch that, who says you can't buy one and get seven free? I'll wrap the whole thing up for you.

> **归寂：**幻胧讨厌脑袋一根筋，无法被她占夺的肉身…她最痛恨品尝人类的勇气了。她最大的弱点就是那既要又要、贪得无厌的态度。

**Asat Pramad:** Phantylia loathes stubborn minds, hosts she can't seize... She abhors the taste of human courage. Her greatest weakness is her insatiable greed, always wanting everything at once.

**Asat Pramad:** Zephyro... He's the perfect warrior.

**Asat Pramad:** But I guess what he fears and hates most is those soulless lunatics, puppets that had their souls snatched by the black sun. To put it bluntly, he's afraid of the dark.

> **归寂：**光逝是头听不懂人话的畜生。啧，畜生当然不是无懈可击的。狗的弱点就是骨头，送它一颗太阳，它保准屁颠屁颠追着跑。

**Asat Pramad:** Luxbane is a beast, doesn't understand human language at all. Beasts aren't flawless, of course. A dog's weakness is bones, and if you show it the sun, it will definitely chase after it.

> **归寂：**星啸，她最反感优美的谐乐了。如果不能被杂音和噪声包围，这个音痴就会坐立不安。

**Asat Pramad:** Celenova, she loathes anything beautifully melodic. Without a haze of noise and distortion around her, she grows restless.

> **归寂：**铸王！我喜欢这老哥。安静，理性，优雅…简直不像干绝灭大君这行的。但我听说他最畏惧的就是现身人前，想必是个社恐了。

**Asat Pramad:** Archforger! A fellow I'm quite fond of: quiet, rational, and elegant... hardly what you'd expect of a Lord Ravager. I hear he's most afraid of appearing before others, probably a social recluse.

**Asat Pramad:** Irontomb... Well, you guys already offed that one.

> **归寂：**至于我嘛。我的弱点也是我的优势：偶然性。

**Asat Pramad:** Now me. My weakness also happens to be my strength: chance.

> **归寂：**我总是抗拒不了在偶然间播下几颗种子，看它们以意想不到的方式生根发芽，最终结出我未曾预想的果实。

**Asat Pramad:** I've never been able to resist planting several seeds on a whim and watching them take root in unexpected ways, and finally bear fruit I never expected.

**Asat Pramad:** So. Satisfied?

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** But then again, who could pass up the chance to gossip about colleagues? Hehehe! Whose weakness do you want to know?

> **归寂：**不过，我一次只回答一个问题。

**Asat Pramad:** But I only answer one question at a time.

> **归寂：**星啸，她最反感优美的谐乐了。如果不能被杂音和噪声包围，这个音痴就会坐立不安。

- ▸ Celenova

**Asat Pramad:** Celenova, she loathes anything beautifully melodic. Without a haze of noise and distortion around her, she grows restless.

- ▸ A different one

**Asat Pramad:** A different one... which Lord Ravager then?

- ▸ Archforger

**Asat Pramad:** Archforger! A fellow I'm quite fond of: quiet, rational, and elegant... hardly what you'd expect of a Lord Ravager. I hear he's most afraid of appearing before others, probably a social recluse.

**Asat Pramad:** Of course, you may ask another question since the last one doesn't count.

> **归寂：**我？我的弱点也是我的优势：偶然性。

- ▸ You!

**Asat Pramad:** Take your time.

> **归寂：**怎么样，满意了吗？

**Asat Pramad:** So. Satisfied?

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** You failed. And even if you'd succeeded... so what? The Lord Ravagers' weaknesses? Knowing wouldn't make a difference.

> **归寂：**你没投中。就算投中了又怎样？绝灭大君的弱点？知道也没什么用。

**Asat Pramad:** Zephyro is scared of the dark, Archforger has social anxiety, Celenova is tone-deaf, and Phantylia has trouble choosing. Haha. Does this help you at all?

> **归寂：**焚风怕黑，铸王社恐，星啸是音痴，幻胧有选择困难症。哈哈，这对你有任何帮助吗？

- ▸ Have you ever been in love?

**(Trailblazer):** Mr. Asat Pramad, have you ever been in love?

> **开拓者：**归寂先生，你谈过恋爱吗？

**Asat Pramad:** What did you just say?

> **归寂：**你说什么？

**(Trailblazer):** Sorry, but I'm really curious. Have you ever fallen in love?

> **开拓者：**抱歉，但我真的很好奇——你、谈过、恋爱吗？

**Asat Pramad:** ...?

> **归寂：**……？

**Asat Pramad:** You have this rare opportunity to speak to a former Navigator, and you're interested in his personal life!?

> **归寂：**一个向前辈领航员提问的珍贵机会，你竟然用来关心他的个人八卦！

- ▸ Are you going to answer or not?
- ▸ What do you care what I ask!

**Asat Pramad:** Hahaha... you really are something else! So intriguing I almost want to throw in the towel and bow out...

> **归寂：**哈哈哈哈…你真是太有趣了！有趣到我都有点想就此放手，直接退赛了——

**(Trailblazer):** So I should roll?

> **开拓者：**那我掷骰子咯？

**Asat Pramad:** Wait, not yet. Don't roll. If you roll a "critical success," I honestly have no idea how I'm supposed to weave a sharp jab at cosmic destiny into something about personal emotions...

> **归寂：**等等，先别投。我真的想不出万一你要是投出了「大成功」，我该怎么在一段涉及私人情感的回答里插入对宇宙命运的讽刺锐评……

**Asat Pramad:** So be it, this one's on me. I'll answer you plainly.

> **归寂：**所以算了，这个问题就当是我免费奉送你了。我就坦率回答你吧。

**Asat Pramad:** I've loved.

> **归寂：**…爱过。

**Asat Pramad:** Of course, I've loved before. But in the end, every planet tied to my past lovers... I destroyed them all.

> **归寂：**我当然，爱过。不过到头来，前任们所在的星球统统被我毁了。

**Asat Pramad:** Not because we broke up, I genuinely cared for them. I just like what I do now even more.

> **归寂：**不是因为分手——我很喜欢对方，但我更喜欢现在这份工作。

**Asat Pramad:** Feels good to get that off my shoulders. They have no complaints either. Whew.

> **归寂：**一旦把话讲开了，前任们也都没什么怨言。挺好的。

**Asat Pramad:** Alright, this won't happen again. Maybe you still have a lot of questions like this. Maybe you'll just ask them directly. But let me make it clear: I don't want to see such questions again.

> **归寂：**好了，下不为例。也许你肚子里还藏着很多这样的问题，也许你会毫不犹豫地把它们都问出来…有言在先，我不想再看到这样的问题了。

- ▸ Is Akivili still alive?

**(Trailblazer):** You said you used to be the navigator. So, is the Aeon of Trailblaze still alive?

> **开拓者：**关于我体内的星核…你知道多少？

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Looks like I have to tell you The Secret Myself and Akivili Must Reveal

> **归寂：**它是希佩所赐下的种子，却又被「开拓」的轨迹所烙印。

**Asat Pramad:** Akivili is dead, but perhaps THEY are still alive, in a way even THEY never expected.

> **归寂：**希佩总是试图让万物同化为一，而阿基维利则不断开辟新的差异。这是两种背道而驰的力量，却在你体内达成了危险的平衡。

**Asat Pramad:** The "fall" of an Aeon is simply a change to the form in which THEY currently exist. No one knows if this is something THEY planned.

> **归寂：**而你的身体…似乎也是为了这个目的被制造出来的——我想这其中隐藏的秘密，星核猎手的人应该比我更清楚。

**Asat Pramad:** According to Nameless who have met Akivili, THEY frequently mention the origin and end, the Tree of Existence, and what lies beyond the Paths.

> **归寂：**星核是命途的碎片，是星神力量与概念的凝结物。

**Asat Pramad:** After all, bound within finite space and time, there is only one place the Aeon of the Trailblaze longs to reach: beyond the universe.

> **归寂：**我想你应该知道了吧？它来自「同谐」。

**Asat Pramad:** And so I witnessed the fall of an Aeon... That power that unveiled the stars and traversed space — in the end, it left nothing but an inconsequential point of light at the edge of existence.

> **归寂：**你体内的这一颗…奇怪，不管它的本质如何，它似乎与「开拓」有着千丝万缕的联系。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** THEY died. Slain by THEIR own desire: to transcend origin and end, to surpass the Tree of Existence, and reach beyond the Paths.

> **归寂：**它是引擎，也是坐标，更是…某种连我也未能完全看透的可能性的载体。

**Asat Pramad:** After all, when the universe has "no roads left to walk," then there's only one place left for the Aeon of Trailblaze to go to.

> **归寂：**遗憾呐，我们人生中大部分时间都要和「失败」为伍的。

**Asat Pramad:** And so I witnessed the fall of an Aeon... That power that unveiled the stars and traversed space — in the end, it left nothing but an inconsequential point of light at the edge of existence.

> **归寂：**星核是一份礼物，一份来自星神的、无与伦比的馈赠。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Ah, you have rolled poorly. Let me think, how do I reply to you?

> **归寂：**它给予你力量，指引你方向，让你成为与众不同的存在。就这么简单。

**Asat Pramad:** Haha, of course THEY are still alive. Is it not said that THEY live within the bodies and in the footsteps of you Nameless?

> **归寂：**这可是个庞大复杂的问题。啊，每个反派都在等这样一个解释自己阴谋的机会——

**Asat Pramad:** No matter how many times the Express crashes, it will always return to its voyage. Surely this is proof that Akivili is still living, yes?

> **归寂：**但是真要从头讲起，恐怕你也不会有耐心听完，对吧？不如你来问自己感兴趣的事情吧？

**Asat Pramad:** Only when you reach Pegana, perhaps, will you finally realize the Trailblaze is nothing but a lie.

> **归寂：**来吧，丢个骰子。让我们用结果说话，看看你能揭开多少秘密。

- ▸ The reason for awakening the Voracity

**(Trailblazer):** Tell me. Why do you want to awaken the Voracity husk?

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Lucky you.

**Asat Pramad:** I awaken the Voracity to devour, of course.

> **归寂：**你是说，那个月亮突然开口许诺「胜者将会成为永久的星神」？

**Asat Pramad:** Even if it's just a fragment sealed in Ahatopia, a husk, the hunger concealed in it will be enough to dim the stars.

> **归寂：**哈哈哈，你未免太高看我了吧。

**Asat Pramad:** I don't care who wins this chess game. What I care about is the moment the winner excitedly reaches out a hand to take their trophy...

> **归寂：**虽然在银河中总有数不胜数的人宣称自己就是阿哈本尊…但我们都明白，能让那枚月亮张口说话的…只有祂自己。

**Asat Pramad:** And a star comes alive, opens its abyssal maw, and swallows the chess pieces, players, and the chess board itself, all to remain in Nihility for all eternity. What a sight! Even the rules themselves would be eaten.

> **归寂：**祂降下神谕，也证明了「欢愉」正时刻审视着这场游戏，而我的所行所为并没有逾越界限。

**Asat Pramad:** No matter who Aha pours THEIR power into at the end of the games, no matter THEIR disguise, it is all for nothing against the awakened Voracity.

> **归寂：**要入侵哈托彼亚，最棘手的莫过于两样东西：将兽蜕牢牢封藏起来的「画中世界」，还有怎么都杀不死的「镇世神木」。

**Asat Pramad:** THEY cannot escape this "devouring" because it is not a duel but... the restoration of balance. It is the consequences and retribution THEY deserve for the sin of theft THEY committed at the dawn of time.

> **归寂：**但是，阿哈从来没有规定过裁判不能成为谒者本身——真奇怪，大家竟然无视这个漏洞长达几千年。

**Asat Pramad:** Though Oroboros does not understand the notion of "revenge," when that ancient hunger, dormant for ages, is sated once more, and the stolen power surges back through THEM, even that blind and witless primordial will shall, in that instant, taste the flavor of "Elation."

> **归寂：**要办到这事简单得出人意料。只要一张面具，在她尚未醒来的时刻，将面具安放在秘庭的神木上就行。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** I awaken the Voracity to devour, of course.

> **归寂：**怎么会？我和满愿之间清清白白。

**Asat Pramad:** Even if it's just a fragment sealed in the core of Ahatopia, a husk, the Voracity concealed in it will be enough to dim the stars.

> **归寂：**像她这样一心复仇的好苗子，根本不需要，也没法参与到任何复杂的计划里。这时候你要做的就是放手，任由她发挥长才。

**Asat Pramad:** I don't care who wins this chess game. What I care about is the moment the winner excitedly reaches out a hand to take their trophy...

> **归寂：**当然，为了推她一把，我赠送了她一件小小的礼物——一块倏忽血肉的碎片。

**Asat Pramad:** And a star comes alive, opens its abyssal maw, and swallows the chess pieces, players, and the chess board itself, all to remain in Nihility for all eternity. What a sight! Even the rules themselves would be eaten.

> **归寂：**就这样？我还指望你挖得更深一些呢。重温自己的工作计划，确实令我感到愉快。

**Asat Pramad:** No matter who Aha pours THEIR power into at the end of the games, no matter THEIR disguise, it is all for nothing against the awakened Voracity.

> **归寂：**关于这场计划，我可以回答一个你最想知道的问题。

**Asat Pramad:** THEY cannot escape this "devouring."

> **归寂：**你是说，那个月亮突然开口许诺「胜者将会成为永久的星神」？

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** I am sorry, but your bad roll will result in a disappointing answer.

> **归寂：**哈哈哈，你未免太高看我了吧。

**Asat Pramad:** I want to awaken the Voracity to create a new and fair world, of course.

> **归寂：**虽然在银河中总有数不胜数的人宣称自己就是阿哈本尊…但我们都明白，能让那枚月亮张口说话的…只有祂自己。

**Asat Pramad:** All lives that have been oppressed and exploited will pass into the ancient Leviathan's belly, and there take on another form: becoming the nourishment of a new world... that is how it will be.

> **归寂：**祂降下神谕，也证明了「欢愉」正时刻审视着这场游戏，而我的所行所为并没有逾越界限。

**Asat Pramad:** Sounds pretty reasonable, doesn't it?

- ▸ The truth about the Phantasmoon Games

**(Trailblazer):** Can you tell me the real truth about the Phantasmoon Games?

**Asat Pramad:** I bet you figured it out long ago, didn't you?

> **归寂：**但是，阿哈从来没有规定过裁判不能成为谒者本身——真奇怪，大家竟然无视这个漏洞长达几千年。

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Oh? Critical success! Then perhaps I should clear things up a bit more.

> **归寂：**要办到这事简单得出人意料。只要一张面具，在她尚未醒来的时刻，将面具安放在秘庭的神木上就行。

**Asat Pramad:** Across countless generations, the Supplicants' Wishpower has gathered here, coursing through the abundant Elwood into the veins of the Path. It soothes the ever-starving husk and keeps it from rising again.

**Asat Pramad:** People believe the Supplicants' miracle was created to please Aha, but this is wrong.

> **归寂：**像她这样一心复仇的好苗子，根本不需要，也没法参与到任何复杂的计划里。这时候你要做的就是放手，任由她发挥长才。

**Asat Pramad:** People believe the markings around the mask measure how much Wishpower each Supplicant has gathered, but they're wrong!

> **归寂：**当然，为了推她一把，我赠送了她一件小小的礼物——一块倏忽血肉的碎片。

**Asat Pramad:** Those markings are telling everyone this: If you want to lull the Voracity into sleep, you must fill them.

> **归寂：**就这样？我还指望你能挖得更深一些呢。重温自己的工作计划，确实令我感到愉快。

**Asat Pramad:** Only when the hunger and thirst of the slumbering beast are satiated with Wishpower, can the beings of Ahatopia struggle on into the next Amber Era. And then Aha will appear to reward this savior, by sharing one minute of THEIR life.

**Asat Pramad:** Everyone is envious of the infinite power of the Aeons and THEIR eternal life.

> **归寂：**你看，这本来是让幕后黑手自白的好桥段，结果被你糟糕的骰运给毁了！难过。

**Asat Pramad:** But who can comprehend THEIR suffering on those Paths, bound by their own authority? Becoming unfeeling instruments of the law, even a god as close to humanity as... "Elation."

**Asat Pramad:** For humans, the prize of these games lasts for only a fleeting minute. But for an Aeon, it's a free and blissful reprieve from the shackles of THEIR Path.

> **归寂：**推理小说中凶手最常说的台词是：「没错，都是我干的。」

**Asat Pramad:** Do you understand now? The Phantasmoon Games are a "holiday for a god."

> **归寂：**看着这天崩地裂的世界，能满心欢喜地说出这句凶手自白，感觉真好。

**Asat Pramad:** Only during such a respite can "Elation," vulnerable and unguarded, be killed.

> **归寂：**大成功？运气太好了吧？

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Across countless generations, the Supplicants' Wishpower has gathered here, coursing through the abundant Elwood into the veins of the Path. It soothes the ever-starving husk and keeps it from rising again.

> **归寂：**啊，艾利欧的「剧团」…他们追求的，可是连星神都未必敢轻易触碰的东西。

**Asat Pramad:** So, people are not acting for Aha's entertainment. They're doing their best to come out of the next Amber Era unscathed.

> **归寂：**他们并非在反抗某个具体的末日，而是在尝试杀死「终末」本身——

**Asat Pramad:** But after thousands of years, this was no longer a game, but a rotten play! On stage, the actors battled. Down below, the audience was numb. The director... well, the director lost interest in their own production, remaining as an empty shell of a body. And then the pitiable process starts all over again.

**Asat Pramad:** There you have it, the truth of the Phantasmoon Games.

> **归寂：**艾利欧所见的，恐怕是一个连「毁灭」都称不上、一切都归于绝对静止与虚无的终点。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** What else could it be? The Phantasmoon Games is just entertainment for Aha.

> **归寂：**星核猎手穿梭于诸界，是在为整个宇宙，寻找一条能够继续演变下去的生路。这目的，可比看上去…悲壮得多，也疯狂得多。

**Asat Pramad:** Aha used the one-minute-as-an-Aeon prize to move the masses around like pieces on a chessboard, delighting in their embarrassing displays.

> **归寂：**这个答案你当然知道。艾利欧…一个能窥见「可能性」分支的存在。

- ▸ The Stellaron inside me

**(Trailblazer):** About the Stellaron inside of me, how much do you know...?

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Interesting... Allow me to attempt to answer this difficult question.

> **归寂：**星核猎手在他的指引下行动，目标明确：收集或干涉特定的星核，以塑造一条他们所期望的「未来」。

**Asat Pramad:** I examined the Stellaron inside your body. It's not a seed of ruin in the usual sense.

> **归寂：**那或许是一个在宏大灾难中保全更多存在的未来，又或许…是满足他们某个私人愿望的未来。他们是命运的「修正者」，尽管动机成谜。

**Asat Pramad:** It was a seed bestowed on you by Xipe, yet branded with the imprint of the Trailblaze.

> **归寂：**星核猎手？一群被力量迷惑、在星神棋局边缘捡拾碎屑的投机者罢了。

**Asat Pramad:** Xipe constantly tries to assimilate all things into one, while Akivili constantly creates new variations. These two forces repel each other, but in your body they have achieved a dangerous equilibrium.

> **归寂：**他们以为自己预见了未来，实则只是被某位星神的力量牵着鼻子走，追逐着一个虚幻的「更好的结局」。

**Asat Pramad:** And your body... it seems to have been created for just this purpose, and it conceals a secret inside it. The Stellaron Hunters likely understand this better than I do.

> **归寂：**至于他们的目的？人类所追求的无非就这两样东西：活下去，或是死得其所。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** The Stellaron is a fragment of a Path, a condensing of Aeonic powers and concepts.

**Asat Pramad:** It comes from Harmony, but surely you already know this?

> **归寂：**我唤醒「贪饕」当然是为了「吞噬」。

**Asat Pramad:** The one in your body... Strange, whatever its essence is, it seems to be intricately linked to the Trailblaze.

> **归寂：**哪怕只是封印在哈托彼亚的一块碎片、一具兽蜕，其中蕴藏的「饥渴」也足够让星辰失色了。

**Asat Pramad:** It's the engine, the coordinates, and... a vessel of possibilities that even I cannot fully see through.

> **归寂：**我不在乎棋盘上谁赢了这局游戏。我在乎的是，当胜者满怀期待地伸出手，准备接过奖杯的瞬间——

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** What a pity that we should live with failure for most of our lives.

> **归寂：**一颗星星活了过来，裂开深渊般的巨口，将棋子、棋手、连同整张棋盘，一同吞入永恒的虚无。那将是连「规则」本身都被嚼碎咽下的景象。

**Asat Pramad:** The Stellaron is a gift, an unparalleled gift from the Aeons.

> **归寂：**无论阿哈在游戏结束时，将自己的力量寄存在谁的躯壳里，无论祂伪装成什么模样——在苏醒的「贪饕」面前，都毫无意义。

**Asat Pramad:** It gives you power, guides you, and makes you unique. It's as simple as that.

> **归寂：**祂无法逃开这场「吞噬」。因为那不是对决，而是…平衡归位。是祂在亘古之前所犯下窃夺之罪应得的报应和后果。

- ▸ The Stellaron Hunters' plan

**(Trailblazer):** I've always been curious... Since the Stellaron Hunters also see possibilities, do you know what they're truly working toward?

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** A critical success? Your luck is off the charts!

**Asat Pramad:** Ah... Elio's troupe. What they're chasing is something even the Aeons might hesitate to touch.

> **归寂：**我唤醒「贪饕」当然是为了「吞噬」。

**Asat Pramad:** They are not fighting against a specific apocalypse, but trying to destroy the Finality itself.

> **归寂：**哪怕只是封印在哈托彼亚核心的一块碎片、一具兽蜕，那其中蕴藏的「饥渴」也足够让星辰失色了。

**Asat Pramad:** To be more exact, they seek to destroy a finale in which every possibility has been frozen.

> **归寂：**我不在乎棋盘上谁赢了这局游戏。我在乎的是，当胜者满怀期待地伸出手，准备接过奖杯的瞬间——

**Asat Pramad:** What Elio has glimpsed may be an end that goes beyond the Destruction itself, a point where all things settle into complete stillness and Nihility.

> **归寂：**一颗星星活了过来，裂开深渊般的巨口，将棋子、棋手、连同整张棋盘，一同吞入永恒的虚无。 那将是连「规则」本身都被嚼碎咽下的景象。

**Asat Pramad:** The Stellaron Hunters traverse the worlds in search of a path that will let the entire universe keep evolving. Their purpose is far more tragic, and far more mad, than it appears.

> **归寂：**无论阿哈在游戏结束时，将自己的力量寄存在谁的躯壳里，无论祂伪装成什么模样——在苏醒的「贪饕」面前，都毫无意义。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Of course you know the answer. Elio... an existence that can see the branching of "possibilities."

> **归寂：**祂无法逃开这场「吞噬」。

**Asat Pramad:** The Stellaron Hunters operate under his guidance. Their goal is clear: to collect or interfere with specific Stellarons to shape the future they desire.

> **归寂：**可惜，可惜…这就是运气。有成功就会有失败。

**Asat Pramad:** A future in which more lives are saved during great calamities perhaps... or a future where they can fulfill their personal wishes. They are the "editors" of fate, even if their motives remain a mystery.

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Stellaron Hunters? They're just a bunch of power-drunk opportunists that wait by the Aeons' chessboard, waiting to catch any falling scraps.

> **归寂：**我想唤醒「贪饕」，当然是为了创造一个全新的、公平的世界。

**Asat Pramad:** They think they've foreseen the future, but in reality, they're merely being led by the nose by a certain Aeon's power, chasing an illusory "better ending."

> **归寂：**所有被压迫、剥削的生命，将去往古兽的腹中，就此以另一种形态存在——成为全新世界的养料…就是这样。

**Asat Pramad:** As for their goal? Humans only pursue these two things: to live, or to die for a reason.

- ▸ I want to know your plan

**(Trailblazer):** What exactly did you do during these Phantasmoon Games?

> **开拓者：**你到底是怎么从列车领航员堕落成绝灭大君的？

**Asat Pramad:** This is a very complicated question. And well, all villains are always waiting for the opportunity to explain their scheme...

> **归寂：**你管这叫「堕落」，我却称之为…「升华」。

**Asat Pramad:** But if I had to start from the beginning, I'm afraid you wouldn't have the patience to listen to the end. Why don't you ask about something you're interested in?

> **归寂：**在二相乐园旅行至今，你喜欢这个「欢愉」的世界吗？

**Asat Pramad:** Come on, roll the die. We'll see how many secrets your roll will earn you.

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Hm! Good throw. Ask away.

> **归寂：**看来咱们所见略同。

- ▸ The Phantasmoon's declaration. That was you, wasn't it?

**Asat Pramad:** You mean when the moon suddenly promised that the "winner would become a permanent Aeon"?

> **归寂：**麻木不仁。这也是绝大多数二相乐园人类内心的真实写照。

**Asat Pramad:** Hahaha! You think too highly of me.

> **归寂：**饱食终日、无所事事的人为无足轻重的观点分歧在网络上咬得头破血流。

**Asat Pramad:** Countless people in the cosmos go around claiming to be Aha THEMSELF. But as we all know, only the Aeon THEMSELF could have made that moon speak.

> **归寂：**足不出户的人类在网络上表演愤怒、哭泣、欢喜…双眼死死盯着银河另一端时刻发生的悲剧，把别人的生死挣扎当成下饭的调料，转过头去又打赏那些博他们一笑的小丑。

**Asat Pramad:** THEIR divine prophecies prove that the Elation is watching these Games at all times, and that my activities have not crossed any boundaries.

> **归寂：**而在银河的另一端，公司正在对伊斯特邦联进行轰炸，人们龟缩在废墟里连一滴干净的饮水也得不到，为明天和死亡哪个先来而颤抖。

- ▸ Are you the one who pushed Evanescia to join the Phantasmoon Games?

**Asat Pramad:** When it comes to infiltrating Ahatopia, the two trickiest obstacles are: the World in Canvas that keeps the husk sealed away, and the unkillable Panarch Elwood.

> **归寂：**这样的宇宙…幸运和不幸的反差，太刺眼了。

**Asat Pramad:** But Aha has never specified that a judge cannot become a Supplicant. Strangely, nobody has ever used this loophole in all these years.

> **归寂：**当然没有！绝灭大君可不是一蹴而就的！

**Asat Pramad:** It's surprisingly easy. All it takes is one mask, which is placed onto the Elwood in the Courtyard before she wakes up.

> **归寂：**我曾和阿基维利一样，相信探索与连接能带来理解，理解能消除隔阂；新发现的世界可以为那些被战火与饥饿摧残的人们带去庇护。

- ▸ You also compelled Fulwish?

**Asat Pramad:** Why would I? My relationship with Fulwish has always been aboveboard.

> **归寂：**但是，所谓开拓，不过是把天真和希望播撒到注定毁灭的土地上。

**Asat Pramad:** There's no need to compel a prospect as driven for revenge as she was. And it's impossible to include anyone like her in any complex scheme. All one could do at this point was set her free and allow her to shine.

> **归寂：**星海之下，每个文明的终点都惊人地相似：在抵达辉煌顶点后，因内部的愚蠢、贪婪或纯粹的偶然而自我毁灭。阿基维利的道路，只是在延缓结局，却无法改变本质。

**Asat Pramad:** Of course, she needed a small gift for motivational purposes, which I provided... a sliver of Shuhu's flesh.

> **归寂：**我看得太多，走了太远。我…再也笑不出来了。

- ▸ I don't have any more questions.

**Asat Pramad:** That's it? I was hoping you'd dig a little deeper. Going over my past schemes is actually quite a treat.

> **归寂：**最后，是「负创神」向我展示的另一条路——「毁灭」。

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** I am willing to answer your most pressing question about this particular scheme.

> **归寂：**在祂的命途里，暴食者和饥饿者，勇敢者和懦弱者——一切不平等的参差有了平等的结局。

- ▸ The Phantasmoon's declaration. That was you, wasn't it?

**Asat Pramad:** You mean when the moon suddenly promised that the "winner would become a permanent Aeon"?

> **归寂：**只有当人们意识到「毁灭」必然到来时，他们的反抗才有了意义，他们的存在才有价值可言——

**Asat Pramad:** Hahaha! You think too highly of me.

**Asat Pramad:** Countless people in the cosmos go around claiming to be Aha THEMSELF. But as we all know, only the Aeon THEMSELF could have made that moon speak.

> **归寂：**从那一刻开始，「我见」死去，「归寂」诞生。

**Asat Pramad:** THEIR divine prophecies prove that the Elation is watching these Games at all times, and that my activities have not crossed any boundaries.

> **归寂：**看看你脚下的二相乐园，还需要解释吗？

- ▸ Are you the one who pushed Evanescia to join the Phantasmoon Games?

**Asat Pramad:** When it comes to infiltrating Ahatopia, the two trickiest obstacles are: the World in Canvas that keeps the husk sealed away, and the unkillable Panarch Elwood.

> **归寂：**这只是这片银河荒诞的缩影之一。饱食终日、无所事事的人为无足轻重的观点分歧在网络上咬得头破血流。

**Asat Pramad:** But Aha has never specified that a judge cannot become a Supplicant. Strangely, nobody has ever used this loophole in all these years.

> **归寂：**足不出户的人类在网络上表演愤怒、哭泣、欢喜…双眼死死盯着银河另一端时刻发生的悲剧，把别人的生死挣扎当成下饭的调料，转过头去又打赏那些博他们一笑的小丑。

**Asat Pramad:** It's surprisingly easy. All it takes is one mask, which is placed onto the Elwood in the Courtyard before she wakes up.

> **归寂：**而在银河的另一端，公司正在对伊斯特邦联进行轰炸，人们龟缩在废墟里连一滴干净的饮水也得不到，为明天和死亡哪个先来而颤抖。

- ▸ You also compelled Fulwish?

**Asat Pramad:** Why would I? My relationship with Fulwish has always been aboveboard.

> **归寂：**这样的宇宙…幸运和不幸的反差，太刺眼了。

**Asat Pramad:** There's no need to compel a prospect as driven for revenge as she was. And it's impossible to include anyone like her in any complex scheme. All one could do at this point was set her free and allow her to shine.

**Asat Pramad:** Of course, she needed a small gift for motivational purposes, which I provided... a sliver of Shuhu's flesh.

> **归寂：**我看得太多，走了太远。我…再也笑不出来了。

**Asat Pramad:** That's it? I was hoping you'd dig a little deeper. Going over my past schemes is actually quite a treat.

> **归寂：**最后，是「负创神」向我展示的另一条路——「毁灭」。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Look, this is where the mastermind is forced to confess. It was supposed to be the good part but alas, your dreadful roll has ruined it! Pity.

> **归寂：**在祂的命途里，暴食者和饥饿者，勇敢者和懦弱者——一切不平等的参差有了平等的结局。

**Asat Pramad:** Looks like your curiosity will have to stay in the realm of speculation.

> **归寂：**从那一刻开始，「我见」死去，「归寂」诞生。

**Asat Pramad:** In detective novels, the murderer always says the line: "Yes, it was me."

> **归寂：**就像投骰失败一样，我成为现在的样子，是因为择业不慎。

**Asat Pramad:** Standing in a world falling apart like this, there's something so satisfying about delivering a killer's confession so gleefully.

> **归寂：**一开始，我是个假面愚者，但我厌倦了「酒馆」里白痴愚者们饱食终日无所事事的玩笑，所以，我离开了。

- ▸ Why embrace Destruction?

**(Trailblazer):** How did you fall from being the Astral Express's Navigator to becoming a Lord Ravager?

(If the roll is a Critical Success)

**Asat Pramad:** You call it falling, but I see it as sublimation.

> **归寂：**之后，我成了无名客。我引领列车踏向一个又一个未知的世界，帮助人们逃离战火和饥馑。

**Asat Pramad:** You've journeyed through Planarcadia. How do you like this world of Elation?

> **归寂：**不过很遗憾，一旦人们堕入安逸与繁荣的蜜糖里，他们就会变成不思进取的苍蝇，在无病呻吟的嗡鸣中腐化堕落。

- ▸ I like it.

**Asat Pramad:** I don't like it.

- ▸ I don't like it.

**Asat Pramad:** Seems like we agree on this point.

> **归寂：**谁能拒绝在上了几百年班、看了几百年人类的丑态以后，把一颗星球点燃？

- ▸ I feel nothing for it.

**Asat Pramad:** Numb and unfeeling. That's the honest reflection of most humans in Planarcadia.

**Asat Pramad:** They spend their time in idle satiety, then peck each other bloody in online arguments over insignificant issues.

> **归寂：**看来，我不得不告诉你《我与阿基维利不得不说的秘密》咯？

**Asat Pramad:** People never leave their homes, but online they are angry, crying, and cheerful. Their eyes are glued to tragedies unfolding across the cosmos, treating others' struggles for life as seasoning for their meals, then turning around to tip the clowns who make them laugh.

**Asat Pramad:** On the other side of the cosmos, the IPC is bombing the Isthmian Confederation. People huddle in the ruins without a single drop of clean drinking water, trembling as they wait to see whether tomorrow or death comes for them first.

> **归寂：**对于星神，「陨落」只是换了一种存在的形式。没人知道这一点是否在祂的计划内。

**Asat Pramad:** In this universe, the contrast between luck and misfortune is too dazzling.

> **归寂：**在见过阿基维利的无名客口中，祂常常会提到始源和终结，提到存在之树和「命途」之外。

- ▸ And so you became a Lord Ravager?
- ▸ And so you decided to bring hell to all?

**Asat Pramad:** Of course not! One does not simply just become a Lord Ravager in an instant.

> **归寂：**毕竟，身处于一个有限的时空里，「开拓」星神最渴望去往的地方有且只有一个：宇宙之外。

**Asat Pramad:** Like Akivili, I once believed that exploring and connecting could create understanding, and understanding could remove division. That the newly discovered worlds could shelter those whose own homes were ravaged by war and hunger.

> **归寂：**于是我经历了一位星神的陨落…那开辟群星、在空间中奔驰的力量——最终只在存在的边界留下一个微不足道的光点。

**Asat Pramad:** But all your Trailblaze does is spread naivete and hope across a land destined to be destroyed.

> **归寂：**祂死了。死于祂的渴望：超越始源和终结，超越存在之树，去向「命途」之外。

**Asat Pramad:** Across the cosmic ocean, all civilizations meet a strikingly similar end: Having climbed to brilliance, they collapse under their own stupidity, avarice, or sheer chance. The path of Akivili only postpones the inevitable, but it cannot change its nature.

> **归寂：**毕竟，当宇宙已经「无路可走」时，「开拓」星神能去往的地方也只剩下一个了。

**Asat Pramad:** I walked too far and saw too much. Afterwards... I could not laugh again.

> **归寂：**于是我经历了一位星神的陨落…那开辟群星、在空间中奔驰的力量——最终只在存在的边界留下一个微不足道的光点。

**Asat Pramad:** Finally, the Blemished One showed me another path: Destruction.

> **归寂：**唔，掷骰失败了。让我想想，我该怎么回答你呢？

**Asat Pramad:** On THEIR Path, the gluttonous and the hungry, the brave and the weak... all inequalities come to an equal end.

> **归寂：**哈哈，祂当然还活着。祂就活在你们这些无名客的身体里、脚步中，不是吗？

**Asat Pramad:** Only when people realize that destruction is imminent do they resist meaningfully, and make their existence worth something....

> **归寂：**无论列车坠落多少次，但它总能再度启航——很难说这不是阿基维利还活着的证据，对吧？

**Asat Pramad:** The rising sun shines on ice sculptures, melts them as if they were never there, even though humans carved them so beautifully, yes?

> **归寂：**也许只有当你们抵达裴伽纳时，才会意识到，「开拓」只是一个谎言。

**Asat Pramad:** From that moment onward, "Isee" died, and "Asat Pramad" was born.

> **开拓者：**告诉我，幻月游戏的真相到底是什么？

(If the roll is a Success)

**Asat Pramad:** Take a look at Planarcadia beneath your feet. Do I need to explain more?

> **归寂：**你应该早就猜到了吧？

**Asat Pramad:** This is just one of the microcosms of this absurd cosmos. They spend their time in idle satiety, then peck each other bloody in online arguments over insignificant issues.

> **归寂：**噢，大成功。那么，不妨让我把话说得更明白一些。

**Asat Pramad:** People never leave their homes, but online they are angry, crying, and cheerful. Their eyes are glued to tragedies unfolding across the cosmos, treating others' struggles for life as seasoning for their meals, then turning around to tip the clowns who make them laugh.

> **归寂：**无数个世代以来，谒者们激起的愿力汇聚于此，经由丰饶神木注入命途的脉络，安抚着永恒饥渴的兽蜕，以免它再度复苏。

**Asat Pramad:** On the other side of the cosmos, the IPC is bombing the Isthmian Confederation. People huddle in the ruins without a single drop of clean drinking water, trembling as they wait to see whether tomorrow or death comes for them first.

**Asat Pramad:** In this universe, the contrast between luck and misfortune is too dazzling.

> **归寂：**人们以为环绕在面具周围的刻度，是在计量每个谒者敛聚的愿力多寡，他们也错了！

**Asat Pramad:** To become a Nameless is to become a witness to more of these ridiculous sights.

> **归寂：**这些标记是在告诉所有人，要让「贪饕」陷入沉睡，你必须填满它们。

**Asat Pramad:** I walked too far and saw too much. Afterwards... I could not laugh again.

> **归寂：**唯有当沉眠兽蜕的饥渴被愿力填满之时，哈托彼亚的生灵才能苟延度过下一个全新的琥珀纪。而阿哈则会现身给予这位救主奖赏——分享神明生命的一分钟。

**Asat Pramad:** Finally, the Blemished One showed me another path: Destruction.

> **归寂：**人人都羡慕星神无限的伟力和祂们永恒的生命。

**Asat Pramad:** On THEIR Path, the gluttonous and the hungry, the brave and the weak... all inequalities come to an equal end.

> **归寂：**但谁能理解，祂们在命途之上驱驰的痛苦，祂们被自己的权柄所束缚，成了无情的法则机器，即便是…「欢愉」这样接近人类的神明。

**Asat Pramad:** From that moment onward, "Isee" died, and "Asat Pramad" was born.

> **归寂：**这场游戏的奖赏，对人类来说是稍纵即逝的一分钟；但对星神而言，却是逃脱命途重负，逍遥自在的假释期。

(If the roll is a Miss)

**Asat Pramad:** Just like a failed throw of the die, I became who I am now because of a careless choice of profession.

> **归寂：**你明白了吧？幻月游戏，就是这样一个「属于神的假期」。

**Asat Pramad:** At first, I was a Masked Fool, but I grew weary of the idiotic Fools in the tavern, feasting and joking away all their days. So, I left.

> **归寂：**也只有在这样的假期中，脆弱且毫无防备的「欢愉」才有可以被杀死的可能。

**Asat Pramad:** Afterwards, I became a Nameless. I led the Express to unknown worlds one after another, helping people to escape war and hunger.

> **归寂：**无数个世代以来，谒者们激起的愿力汇聚于此，经由丰饶神木注入命途的脉络，安抚着永恒饥渴的兽蜕，以免它再度复苏。

**Asat Pramad:** But unfortunately, once people fall into the sweetness of comfort and prosperity, they become like aimless flies, droning with empty self-pity as they slowly rot.

> **归寂：**所以，人们并非在为取悦阿哈而行动，他们是在为自己度过下一个安然无恙的琥珀纪而奋力表演。

**Asat Pramad:** But luckily for me, I've landed on a vocation worthy of a lifetime's devotion: Lord Ravager!

> **归寂：**但数千年过去，这已经不再是游戏了，而是一场腐烂的戏剧！演员在台上搏命，观众在台下麻木，而导演…导演对自己的作品失去了兴趣，只剩下空洞的神躯，重复可悲的流程。

**Asat Pramad:** Who could resist setting a planet ablaze after centuries on the job, watching humanity at its worst?

**Asat Pramad:** If you were in my position, you wouldn't be able to resist either!

> **归寂：**还能为什么？幻月游戏只是一个取悦阿哈的游戏罢了。

*(If the roll is a Critical Success, first time only)*

**Asat Pramad:** Hey, that's a critical success!

*(After each question that has been answered, respectively)*

**Asat Pramad:** Seven questions left.

> **归寂：**还剩下七个问题。

**Asat Pramad:** You may ask six more questions.

> **归寂：**你还可以问六个问题。

**Asat Pramad:** Five left now.

> **归寂：**现在，还有五个问题。

**Asat Pramad:** Four questions left. Keep going.

> **归寂：**还剩四个问题，继续吧。

**Asat Pramad:** You may still ask me three more questions.

> **归寂：**你还可以问三个问题。

**Asat Pramad:** Only two questions left.

> **归寂：**只有两个问题了。

*(After seven questions have been answered)*

**Asat Pramad:** Final question. Think carefully, what do you want to ask?

> **归寂：**最后一个问题。想好要问什么了吗？

**(Trailblazer):** There's something I've thought about from the start.

> **开拓者：**我从一开始就已经想好了。

- ▸ Is Himeko still alive?

**(Trailblazer):** Tell me... is Himeko still alive? Do you really have no feelings for your daughter?

> **开拓者：**告诉我…姬子，还活着吗？你真的对自己的女儿毫无感情吗？

*(If the roll is a Critical Success)*

**Asat Pramad:** Aha! This is the question I've been waiting to answer, what a stroke of luck.

> **归寂：**啊哈！我一直在等待回答这个问题，简直是天遂人愿。

*(If the roll is a Success)*

**Asat Pramad:** Hearing that question is a relief.

> **归寂：**听到这个问题，我竟然有些欣慰。

**Asat Pramad:** Pay no mind to the roll, I'll be honest with you.

> **归寂：**无论你投出什么样的结果，我都会坦诚地回答你。

*(If the roll is a Miss)*

**Asat Pramad:** Tch... your luck really is awful. Of all the questions, you had to fail on the one I most wanted to answer.

> **归寂：**啧…你的运气还真是不妙。在这个我最想回答的问题上，竟然投出了失败。

**Asat Pramad:** Pay no mind to the roll, I'll be honest with you.

> **归寂：**无论你投出什么样的结果，我都会坦诚地回答你。

**Asat Pramad:** Of course Himeko is still alive. At least on the stage I've meticulously prepared for her. She will play an important role, and is now awaiting her curtain call.

> **归寂：**姬子当然还活着。至少在我为她精心准备的「舞台」上，她会扮演一个重要的角色，等待着谢幕时刻的到来。

**Asat Pramad:** Life is like this, one chance journey after another, one whim after another... playing someone's "father," becoming someone's "friend"... and at times, someone's "lover."

> **归寂：**人生就是这样，一次次偶然的旅行，一场场偶发兴致的扮演，扮演某人的「父亲」、成为某人的「朋友」…与某人结为「爱侣」。

**Asat Pramad:** Then, by a twist of fate, I let a girl with no future begin to dream of the stars, and of lands yet to be reached.

> **归寂：**在阴差阳错中，我让一个本没有未来的女孩拥有了对星空和未抵之地的渴望。

**Asat Pramad:** I have never understood why the seeds I planted by chance would always take root in unexpected ways. I never expected them to bear fruit such as Graphia, or Himeko...

> **归寂：**我始终想不明白，为何我偶然播下的种子，总会以意想不到的方式生根发芽，最终结出了我未曾预想的果实…绘世也罢，姬子也好。

**Asat Pramad:** It feels like a curse, like fate's cruelest mockery of me.

> **归寂：**这简直就是一种诅咒，又像是命运对我最大的嘲弄。

**Asat Pramad:** Just like that night fifteen years ago. I had the chance to crush the Express again, but I hesitated.

> **归寂：**就像在十五年前那个夜晚，我本来有机会再度击坠列车。但，我犹豫了。

- ▸ You couldn't bear to do it?
- ▸ Because you used to be its Navigator?

**Asat Pramad:** No, no to both.

> **归寂：**不，都不是。

**Asat Pramad:** I've never fully understood that lapse in judgment. I still ask myself why.

> **归寂：**我一直不明白，那一念之差究竟是为什么。

**(Trailblazer):** No matter the reason, you caused a huge mess for your colleagues.

> **开拓者：**无论为什么，你可是给你的同事们制造了一个超级大麻烦。

**Asat Pramad:** A few weeks ago, Himeko returned to her hometown and brought you back with her.

> **归寂：**直到几周前，姬子再度回到了故乡——她带回了你。

**Asat Pramad:** That was the moment I finally understood why I didn't shoot down the Express fifteen years ago. It had nothing to do with acting as a father, or Trailblazing, but...

> **归寂：**那一刻，我终于明白了，为什么十五年前我没有击落列车——既不是为了「父亲」的身份，也不是为了开拓，而是……

**Asat Pramad:** But for a destruction that spanned fifteen years, to set her on a journey to gain what was most precious, only to shatter it all in the cruelest way.

> **归寂：**而是为了一场跨越十五年的毁灭，为了让她踏上旅途得到至为珍贵之物，再用最残忍的方式毁灭这一切。

**Asat Pramad:** What truly matters is this: I will have you bring an end to this world, here and now. It's the most vicious joke I can play on Planarcadia, on myself, and on the fate of the universe.

> **归寂：**重要的是，我要让你在这里，为这个世界画上句号。这是我能给予二相乐园，给予自身，给予宇宙命运最恶毒的玩笑。

**Asat Pramad:** Take a guess. Will this joke amuse Aha?

> **归寂：**你猜猜，这个玩笑能不能让阿哈满意？

- ▸ You are incorrigible!
- ▸ Have me... bring this world to an end?

**(Trailblazer):** You want to use my Stellaron...

> **开拓者：**…你想利用我的星核。

**Asat Pramad:** The worlds the Astral Express deals with, how could they be without a Stellaron? ...BOOM!!

> **归寂：**星穹列车打交道的世界，又怎么少得了一颗星核呢——BOOM！！

**Asat Pramad:** Fifteen years ago, during the Bloodstained Games, all the Supplicants died violent deaths. Then, the husk ran out of Wishpower, and grew agitated, craving a blood sacrifice.

> **归寂：**十五年前的血涂游戏，所有谒者死于非命。得不到愿力滋养的兽蜕开始不安躁动，渴求血食祭祀。

**Asat Pramad:** Fifteen years later, a hero from the Express shone brightly throughout the Games. His/Her Stellaron, overflowing with Wishpower, the perfect sacrificial piece!

> **归寂：**十五年后，一位列车英雄在这场游戏中出尽风头。他/她那盛满愿力的星核，真是再好不过的祭品了！

**Asat Pramad:** Alright. Now that I've answered the eight questions, I now need to...

> **归寂：**好了，八个问题已过，接下来我要——

**Asat Pramad:** Hmph! Kids these days...

> **归寂：**啧，现在的年轻人……

**Asat Pramad:** Show some respect, and put your phone away when your elder is talking!

> **归寂：**老前辈和你说话的时候不要玩手机！

*(Asat Pramad's phone gets a message notification)*

### Ask Asat Pramad the ninth question

> *Himeko and the Astral Express are on their way. Asat Pramad's scheme has fallen apart! I've put up with you long enough, you rotten old die. Now, let's end this ridiculous farce with our fists!*

*(Talk to Asat Pramad)*

- ▸ Less talk, more action

(Enter Exploratory Excursion Cārvāka, Eternal Superstar)

**Asat Pramad:** What kind of nonsense is that? Boring.

> **归寂：**…但是，注销一位老人的养老金账户这种事情，她的良心不会痛吗？！

**(Trailblazer):** You treat other people's lives and suffering like numbers on a die, toying with them at will, so why do you fall apart when it's your turn?

> **开拓者：**你把别人的生命和痛苦，全都当成骰子上的数字随意拨弄。轮到自己怎么就破防了？

**Asat Pramad:** Your curiosity is commendable, but you've already used up all your questions. So, I won't be answering anything you ask.

> **帕姆：**说好了的帕，「无名客，一个都不许少」！

> **姬子：**毕竟，绒绒号的主角还没上车呢！

> **三月七：**让这家伙见识见识，什么才是真正的无名客！

**Asat Pramad:** Here we go! Companions have entered the ring, and the supporting cast has taken the stage! The daughter turns her blade on her father!

> **归寂：**来了来了！同伴到场，配角亮相！女儿对父亲刀剑相向！

(When Lord of Saṃvartasthāyi, Asat Pramad is reduced to below 50% HP)

**Asat Pramad:** Hit me with all your talk about friendship and justice!

> **归寂：**你们有什么友情啊正义啊，全都冲我来吧！

(When Lord of Saṃvartasthāyi, Asat Pramad enters Phase II)

**Asat Pramad:** Those who pass the challenge are worthy of a reward.

> **归寂：**通过挑战的生灵，应得赏赐。

**Asat Pramad:** What I mean is, you've earned the right to face... Phase! Two!

> **归寂：**我的意思是，你们配得上二、阶、段！

**Asat Pramad:** Ladies and gentlemen, may I present to you: my second mask!

**Asat Pramad:** They say luck is a form of strength. Come on! Let's see your strength.

> **归寂：**运气也是实力的一部分。来！看看各位实力如何？

> *Every journey is destined to come with loss, just as a child will one day grow up overnight. So don't be afraid. You will travel farther than I ever did, farther than any of the Nameless before you. Keep moving forward, children of the Express. No matter when, I will be by your side, all the way to the end of the universe.*

### Return to the battlefield and face the Lord of Saṃvartasthāyi

> *Set forth once more, Akivili. Carry the wishes of all life in the cosmos. Ignite the engine that ran upon the silver rail. Trailblaze beyond the borders of fate itself. May this journey lead us starward.*

*(Interact with the campfire)*

**(Trailblazer) from the Future?:** Countless shooting stars streak across this night sky. If you can pick the right one...

> **未来的开拓者？：**无数流星划过今夜的天空，如果选中了正确的那一颗……

> **未来的开拓者？：**它将把你的愿望带向千百个世界。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Trailblazer:** You must become used to loss in order to continue onward?

*(Begin battle against Lord of Saṃvartasthāyi, Asat Pramad ×20*

### Check on your companions

> *Is it over? Asat Pramad has been defeated by the combined assault of the Express Crew. There shouldn't be any major threats for a while. Looking around, Planarcadia lies in ruins. The rebuilding effort will likely take quite some time. But at the very least, everyone can finally breathe a sigh of relief.*

**Himeko:** It's all over. A lost navigator has reached his final destination.

> **姬子：**你听见人们的笑声了么？他们正在离开废墟，再度启程。
